```
   119→    is_reuse: bool,
   120→    services: TriageServices,
   121→) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
   122→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   123→    reflect_stage: dict = {
   124→        "stage": "reflect",
   125→        "report": report,
   126→        "cited_ids": sorted(cited_ids),
   127→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   128→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   129→        "missing_issue_ids": missing_ids,
   130→        "duplicate_issue_ids": duplicate_ids,
   131→        "recurring_dims": recurring_dims,
   132→    }
   133→    if disposition_ledger:
   134→        reflect_stage["disposition_ledger"] = [d.to_dict() for d in disposition_ledger]
   135→    stages["reflect"] = reflect_stage
   136→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   137→        reflect_stage["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   138→        reflect_stage["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   139→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   140→
   141→    services.save_plan(plan)
   142→    services.append_log_entry(
   143→        plan,
   144→        "triage_reflect",
   145→        actor="user",
   146→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "reuse": is_reuse, "recurring_dims": recurring_dims},
   147→    )
   148→    services.save_plan(plan)
   149→    return reflect_stage, cleared
   150→
   151→
   152→def _cmd_stage_reflect(
   153→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   154→    *,
   155→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current issues against completed work."""
   158→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   159→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   160→
   161→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   162→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   163→    state = runtime.state
   164→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   165→
   166→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   167→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   171→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   172→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   173→
   174→    report, is_reuse = resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   175→    if not report:
   176→        _print_reflect_report_requirement()
   177→        return
   178→
   179→    submission = _validate_reflect_submission(
   180→        report=report,
   181→        plan=plan,
   182→        state=state,
   183→        stages=stages,
   184→        attestation=attestation,
   185→        services=resolved_services,
   186→    )
   187→    if submission is None:
   188→        return
   189→    (
   190→        triage_input, issue_count, recurring, recurring_dims,
   191→        cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids, disposition_ledger,
   192→    ) = submission
   193→    reflect_stage, cleared = _persist_reflect_stage(
   194→        plan=plan,
   195→        meta=meta,
   196→        stages=stages,
   197→        report=report,
   198→        issue_count=issue_count,
   199→        cited_ids=cited_ids,
   200→        missing_ids=missing_ids,
   201→        duplicate_ids=duplicate_ids,
   202→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   203→        disposition_ledger=disposition_ledger,
   204→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   205→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   206→        services=resolved_services,
   207→    )
   208→
   209→    print_reflect_result(
   210→        issue_count=issue_count,
   211→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   212→        recurring=recurring,
   213→        report=report,
   214→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   215→        cleared=cleared,
   216→        stages=stages,
   217→    )
   218→
   219→
   220→def cmd_stage_reflect(
   221→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   222→    *,
   223→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   224→) -> None:
   225→    """Public entrypoint for reflect stage recording."""
   226→    _cmd_stage_reflect(args, services=services)
```

```
1→"""Stage-gate and coverage helpers for triage command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS
     7→
     8→from ..helpers import cluster_issue_ids, live_active_triage_issue_ids
     9→
    10→
    11→def _require_triage_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    12→    """Require at least one triage stage ID to be present in queue for an action."""
    13→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    14→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
    15→        return True
    16→    print(colorize(f"  No triage stage in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    17→    return False
    18→
    19→
    20→def _validate_stage_report(
    21→    report: str | None,
    22→    *,
    23→    stage: str,
    24→    min_chars: int,
    25→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    26→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    27→) -> str | None:
    28→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    29→    if not report:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    31→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    32→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    33→        return None
    34→    cleaned = report.strip()
    35→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    39→                "red",
    40→            )
    41→        )
    42→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    43→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    44→        return None
    45→    return cleaned
    46→
    47→
    48→def unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    49→    """Return clusters with issues that are missing required enrichment.
    50→
    51→    Requirements:
    52→    - Every cluster needs a description and at least one action_step.
    53→    - Small clusters (< 5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue,
    54→      so each item has a concrete plan. Large clusters (>= 5) just need
    55→      steps overall (cluster-level plan is sufficient).
    56→    """
    57→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    58→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    59→        issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    60→        if not issue_ids:
    61→            continue
    62→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    63→            continue
    64→        missing: list[str] = []
    65→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    66→            missing.append("description")
    67→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    68→        issue_count = len(issue_ids)
    69→        if not steps:
    70→            missing.append("action_steps")
    71→        elif issue_count < 5 and len(steps) < issue_count:
    72→            missing.append(
    73→                f"action_steps (have {len(steps)}, need >= {issue_count} for small cluster)"
    74→            )
    75→        if missing:
    76→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    77→    return gaps
    78→
    79→
    80→def unclustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Return review issue IDs that aren't in any manual cluster.
    82→
    83→    When *state* is provided, uses open review/concerns issues from state
    84→    (the canonical source). Falls back to scanning queue_order for backwards
    85→    compatibility.
    86→    """
    87→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    88→    clustered_ids: set[str] = set()
    89→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    90→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    91→            clustered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    92→    skipped_ids = {
    93→        fid for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}).keys() if isinstance(fid, str)
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    if state is not None:
    97→        review_ids = [
    98→            fid for fid, finding in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    99→            if finding.get("status") == "open"
   100→            and finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   101→        ]
   102→        frozen_ids = (plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}) or {}).get("active_triage_issue_ids")
   103→        if isinstance(frozen_ids, list) and frozen_ids:
   104→            frozen_id_set = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   105→            review_ids = [fid for fid in review_ids if fid in frozen_id_set]
   106→    else:
   107→        review_ids = [
   108→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   110→            and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   111→        ]
   112→
   113→    return [
   114→        fid for fid in review_ids
   115→        if fid not in clustered_ids and fid not in skipped_ids
   116→    ]
   117→
```

```
1→"""Organize stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→from ..display.dashboard import print_organize_result
    10→from ..helpers import (
    11→    count_log_activity_since,
    12→    has_triage_in_queue,
    13→    open_review_ids_from_state,
    14→)
    15→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    16→from ..validation.core import (
    17→    ReflectAutoConfirmDeps,
    18→    _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize,
    19→    _clusters_enriched_or_error,
    20→    _manual_clusters_or_error,
    21→    _organize_report_or_error,
    22→    _require_reflect_stage_for_organize,
    23→    _unclustered_review_issues_or_error,
    24→    _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error,
    25→)
    26→from .records import record_organize_stage
    27→
    28→
    29→def _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
    30→    *,
    31→    plan: dict,
    32→    stages: dict,
    33→    manual_clusters: list[str],
    34→    open_review_ids: set[str],
    35→    is_reuse: bool,
    36→    attestation: str | None,
    37→) -> bool:
    38→    """Require enough cluster operations after reflect unless explicitly attested."""
    39→    if not open_review_ids:
    40→        return True
    41→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
    42→    if not reflect_ts or is_reuse:
    43→        return True
    44→    activity = count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
    45→    cluster_ops = sum(
    46→        activity.get(key, 0)
    47→        for key in ("cluster_create", "cluster_add", "cluster_update", "cluster_remove")
    48→    )
    49→    min_ops = max(3, len(manual_clusters))
    50→    if cluster_ops >= min_ops:
    51→        return True
    52→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
    53→        print(
    54→            colorize(
    55→                f"  Note: only {cluster_ops} cluster op(s) logged (expected {min_ops}+). "
    56→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
    57→                "yellow",
    58→            )
    59→        )
    60→        return True
    61→    print(
    62→        colorize(
    63→            f"  Cannot organize: only {cluster_ops} cluster operation(s) logged "
    64→            f"since reflect (need {min_ops}+).",
    65→            "red",
    66→        )
    67→    )
    68→    print(
    69→        colorize(
    70→            "  Cluster operations (create/add/update/remove) are logged automatically\n"
    71→            "  when you use the CLI. Did you create clusters, add issues, and enrich them?",
    72→            "dim",
    73→        )
    74→    )
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why fewer ops are expected" (40+ chars).',
    78→            "dim",
    79→        )
    80→    )
    81→    return False
    82→
    83→
    84→def _validate_organize_submission(
    85→    *,
    86→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    87→    plan: dict,
    88→    state: dict,
    89→    stages: dict,
    90→    report: str | None,
    91→    attestation: str | None,
    92→    is_reuse: bool,
    93→    services: TriageServices,
    94→) -> tuple[list[str], str] | None:
    95→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    96→    triage_input = services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    97→    if not _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
    98→        args=args,
    99→        plan=plan,
   100→        stages=stages,
   101→        attestation=attestation,
   102→        deps=ReflectAutoConfirmDeps(
   103→            triage_input=triage_input,
   104→            detect_recurring_patterns_fn=services.detect_recurring_patterns,
   105→            save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
   106→        ),
   107→    ):
   108→        return None
   109→
   110→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids)
   111→    if manual_clusters is None:
   112→        return None
   113→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   114→        return None
   115→    if not _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan, state):
   116→        return None
   117→    if not _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   118→        plan=plan, stages=stages,
   119→    ):
   120→        return None
   121→    if not _enforce_cluster_activity_for_organize(
   122→        plan=plan,
   123→        stages=stages,
   124→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   125→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   126→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→    ):
   129→        return None
   130→
   131→    normalized_report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   132→    if normalized_report is None:
   133→        return None
   134→
   135→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   136→        format_evidence_failures,
   137→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    cluster_ref_failures = validate_report_references_clusters(normalized_report, manual_clusters)
   141→    if cluster_ref_failures:
   142→        print(
   143→            colorize(
   144→                format_evidence_failures(cluster_ref_failures, stage_label="organize"),
   145→                "red",
   146→            )
   147→        )
   148→        return None
   149→    return manual_clusters, normalized_report
   150→
   151→
   152→def _persist_organize_stage(
   153→    *,
   154→    plan: dict,
   155→    meta: dict,
   156→    report: str,
   157→    open_review_ids: set[str],
   158→    existing_stage: dict | None,
   159→    is_reuse: bool,
   160→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   161→    services: TriageServices,
   162→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   163→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   164→    cleared = record_organize_stage(
   165→        stages,
   166→        report=report,
   167→        issue_count=len(open_review_ids),
   168→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   169→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   170→    )
   171→    services.save_plan(plan)
   172→    services.append_log_entry(
   173→        plan,
   174→        "triage_organize",
   175→        actor="user",
   176→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   177→    )
   178→    services.save_plan(plan)
   179→    return cleared, stages
   180→
   181→
   182→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   183→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   184→    *,
   185→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment."""
   188→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   189→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   190→
   191→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   192→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   193→
   194→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   195→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   196→        return
   197→
   198→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   199→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   200→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   201→
   202→    is_reuse = False
   203→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   204→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   205→        is_reuse = True
   206→
   207→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   211→    state = runtime.state
   212→    open_review_ids = open_review_ids_from_state(state)
   213→
   214→    validated = _validate_organize_submission(
   215→        args=args,
   216→        plan=plan,
   217→        state=state,
   218→        stages=stages,
   219→        report=report,
   220→        attestation=attestation,
   221→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   222→        services=resolved_services,
   223→    )
   224→    if validated is None:
   225→        return
   226→    manual_clusters, normalized_report = validated
   227→    cleared, stages = _persist_organize_stage(
   228→        plan=plan,
   229→        meta=meta,
   230→        report=normalized_report,
   231→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   232→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   233→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   234→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   235→        services=resolved_services,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    print_organize_result(
   239→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        report=normalized_report,
   242→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   243→        cleared=cleared,
   244→        stages=stages,
   245→    )
   246→
   247→
   248→def cmd_stage_organize(
   249→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   250→    *,
   251→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   252→) -> None:
   253→    """Public entrypoint for organize stage recording."""
   254→    _cmd_stage_organize(args, services=services)
   255→
   256→
   257→__all__ = [
   258→    "_cmd_stage_organize",
   259→    "cmd_stage_organize",
   260→]
```

```
1→"""Sense-check stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→
    12→from .records import record_sense_check_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    13→from ..validation.enrich_quality import evaluate_enrich_quality
    14→from ..validation.enrich_checks import (
    15→    _steps_missing_issue_refs,
    16→    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    17→    _steps_with_vague_detail,
    18→    _steps_without_effort,
    19→    _underspecified_steps,
    20→)

    22→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    23→from .enrich import ColorizeFn
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    27→class SenseCheckStageDeps:
    28→    has_triage_in_queue: Callable[[dict], bool] = has_triage_in_queue
    29→    resolve_reusable_report: Callable[[str | None, dict | None], tuple[str | None, bool]] = (
    30→        resolve_reusable_report
    31→    )
    32→    record_sense_check_stage: Callable[..., list[str]] = record_sense_check_stage
    33→    colorize: ColorizeFn = colorize
    34→    default_triage_services: Callable[[], TriageServices] = default_triage_services
    35→    print_cascade_clear_feedback: Callable[[list[str], dict], None] = print_cascade_clear_feedback
    36→    get_project_root: Callable[[], Path] | None = None
    37→    underspecified_steps: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _underspecified_steps
    38→    steps_missing_issue_refs: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _steps_missing_issue_refs
    39→    steps_with_bad_paths: Callable[[dict, Path], list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]] = _steps_with_bad_paths
    40→    steps_with_vague_detail: Callable[[dict, Path], list[tuple[str, int, str]]] = _steps_with_vague_detail
    41→    steps_without_effort: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _steps_without_effort
    42→
    43→
    44→def _sense_check_report(
    45→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    46→    stages: dict,
    47→    *,
    48→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps,
    49→) -> tuple[str | None, bool, dict | None]:
    50→    existing_stage = stages.get("sense-check")
    51→    report = getattr(args, "report", None)
    52→    report, is_reuse = deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    53→    return report, is_reuse, existing_stage
    54→
    55→
    56→def _sense_check_quality_problems(
    57→    plan: dict,
    58→    *,
    59→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps,
    60→) -> list[str]:
    61→    get_project_root = deps.get_project_root
    62→    if get_project_root is None:
    63→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    64→
    65→    repo_root = get_project_root()
    66→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
    67→        plan,
    68→        repo_root,
    69→        phase_label="sense-check",
    70→        bad_paths_severity="failure",
    71→        missing_effort_severity="failure",
    72→        include_missing_issue_refs=True,
    73→        include_vague_detail=True,
    74→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
    75→    )
    76→    return [issue.message for issue in quality_report.failures]
    77→
    78→
    79→def _print_sense_check_problems(
    80→    problems: list[str],
    81→    *,
    82→    colorize_fn: ColorizeFn,
    83→) -> None:
    84→    print(colorize_fn("  Cannot record sense-check — plan still has issues:", "red"))
    85→    for problem in problems:
    86→        print(colorize_fn(f"    • {problem}", "yellow"))
    87→    print(colorize_fn("  Fix these before recording the sense-check stage.", "dim"))
    88→
    89→
    90→def _report_length_ok(report: str | None, *, colorize_fn: ColorizeFn) -> bool:
    91→    if not report:
    92→        print(colorize_fn("  --report is required for --stage sense-check.", "red"))
    93→        print(
    94→            colorize_fn(
    95→                "  Describe what the content and structure subagents found and fixed.",
    96→                "dim",
    97→            )
    98→        )
    99→        return False
   100→    if len(report) < 100:
   101→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   102→        return False
   103→    return True
   104→
   105→
   106→def _sense_check_evidence_failures(
   107→    report: str,
   108→    *,
   109→    plan: dict,
   110→    state: dict,
   111→) -> tuple[list[object], list[object]]:
   112→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   113→        validate_report_has_file_paths,
   114→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   115→    )
   116→    from ..helpers import manual_clusters_with_issues
   117→
   118→    failures: list[object] = []
   119→    if open_review_ids_from_state(state):
   120→        failures.extend(validate_report_has_file_paths(report) or [])
   121→
   122→    cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   123→    if cluster_names:
   124→        failures.extend(validate_report_references_clusters(report, cluster_names) or [])
   125→
   126→    blocking = [failure for failure in failures if failure.blocking]
   127→    advisory = [failure for failure in failures if not failure.blocking]
   128→    return blocking, advisory
   129→
   130→
   131→def _print_evidence_failures(
   132→    failures: list[object],
   133→    *,
   134→    stage_label: str,
   135→    colorize_fn: ColorizeFn,
   136→    style: str,
   137→) -> None:
   138→    from .evidence_parsing import format_evidence_failures
   139→
   140→    if not failures:
   141→        return
   142→    print(colorize_fn(format_evidence_failures(failures, stage_label=stage_label), style))
   143→
   144→
   145→def run_stage_sense_check(
   146→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   147→    *,
   148→    services: TriageServices | None,
   149→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps | None = None,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    """Record the SENSE-CHECK stage after rerunning enrich-level validations."""
   152→    resolved_deps = deps or SenseCheckStageDeps()
   153→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   154→
   155→    resolved_services = services or resolved_deps.default_triage_services()
   156→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   157→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   158→
   159→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   160→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to sense-check.", "yellow"))
   161→        return
   162→
   163→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   164→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   165→    report, is_reuse, existing_stage = _sense_check_report(
   166→        args,
   167→        stages,
   168→        deps=resolved_deps,
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    if not stages.get("enrich", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   172→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot sense-check: enrich stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   173→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    problems = _sense_check_quality_problems(plan, deps=resolved_deps)
   177→    if problems:
   178→        _print_sense_check_problems(problems, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize)
   179→        return
   180→
   181→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All enrich-level checks pass after sense-check.", "green"))
   182→
   183→    if not _report_length_ok(report, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize):
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    blocking_ev, advisory_ev = _sense_check_evidence_failures(report, plan=plan, state=state)
   187→    if blocking_ev:
   188→        _print_evidence_failures(
   189→            blocking_ev,
   190→            stage_label="sense-check",
   191→            colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize,
   192→            style="red",
   193→        )
   194→        return
   195→    if advisory_ev:
   196→        _print_evidence_failures(
   197→            advisory_ev,
   198→            stage_label="sense-check",
   199→            colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize,
   200→            style="yellow",
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   204→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_sense_check_stage(
   205→        stages,
   206→        report=report,
   207→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   208→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   209→    )
   210→
   211→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   212→
   213→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   214→        plan,
   215→        "triage_sense_check",
   216→        actor="user",
   217→        detail={"reuse": is_reuse},
   218→    )
   219→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   220→
   221→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Sense-check stage recorded.", "green"))
   222→    if is_reuse:
   223→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Sense-check data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   224→        if cleared:
   225→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   226→    else:
   227→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the sense-check.", "yellow"))
   228→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check", "dim"))
   229→
   230→
   231→def cmd_stage_sense_check(
   232→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   233→    *,
   234→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   235→) -> None:
   236→    """Public entrypoint for sense-check stage recording."""
   237→    run_stage_sense_check(args, services=services)
```

```
   215→def run_stage_enrich(
   216→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   217→    *,
   218→    services: TriageServices | None,
   219→    deps: EnrichStageDeps | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Record the ENRICH stage with validation and optional auto-confirm."""
   222→    resolved_deps = deps or EnrichStageDeps()
   223→    resolved_services, plan, state, stages, report, attestation = _resolve_enrich_stage_context(
   224→        args=args,
   225→        services=services,
   226→        deps=resolved_deps,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   230→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to enrich.", "yellow"))
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    existing_stage = stages.get("enrich")
   234→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   235→
   236→    if not resolved_deps.require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages):
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    if not _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        stages=stages,
   242→        attestation=attestation,
   243→        services=resolved_services,
   244→        deps=resolved_deps,
   245→    ):
   246→        return
   247→
   248→    if not is_reuse and not _require_cluster_update_activity(
   249→        plan=plan,
   250→        state=state,
   251→        stages=stages,
   252→        attestation=attestation,
   253→        deps=resolved_deps,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    get_project_root = resolved_deps.get_project_root
   258→    if get_project_root is None:
   259→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   260→
   261→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
   262→        plan,
   263→        get_project_root(),
   264→        phase_label="enrich",
   265→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
   266→        missing_effort_severity="warning",
   267→        include_missing_issue_refs=False,
   268→        include_vague_detail=False,
   269→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
   270→    )
   271→    underspec = quality_report.failure("underspecified")
   272→    total_bare = underspec.total if underspec else 0
   273→
   274→    if underspec:
   275→        _print_underspecified_step_error(underspec.rows, deps=resolved_deps)
   276→        return
   277→
   278→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   279→    _print_enrich_warnings(report=quality_report, deps=resolved_deps)
   280→
   281→    report = resolved_deps.enrich_report_or_error(report)
   282→    if report is None:
   283→        return
   284→
   285→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   286→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   287→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_enrich_stage(
   288→        stages,
   289→        report=report,
   290→        shallow_count=total_bare,
   291→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   292→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   293→    )
   294→
   295→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   296→
   297→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   298→        plan,
   299→        "triage_enrich",
   300→        actor="user",
   301→        detail={"shallow_count": total_bare, "reuse": is_reuse},
   302→    )
   303→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   304→
   305→    print(
   306→        resolved_deps.colorize(
   307→            f"  Enrich stage recorded: {total_bare} step(s) still without detail.",
   308→            "green",
   309→        )
   310→    )
   311→    if is_reuse:
   312→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Enrich data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   313→        if cleared:
   314→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   315→    else:
   316→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the enrichment.", "yellow"))
   317→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   318→
   319→    resolved_deps.print_user_message(
   320→        "Enrich recorded. Before confirming — check the subagent's"
   321→        " work. Could a developer who has never seen this code"
   322→        " execute every step without asking a question? Every step"
   323→        " needs: file path, specific location, specific action."
   324→        " 'Refactor X' fails. 'Extract lines 45-89 into Y' passes."
   325→    )
   326→
   327→
   328→def cmd_stage_enrich(
   329→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   330→    *,
   331→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   332→) -> None:
   333→    """Public entrypoint for enrich stage recording."""
   334→    run_stage_enrich(args, services=services)
   335→
   336→
   337→__all__ = ["ColorizeFn", "EnrichStageDeps", "cmd_stage_enrich", "run_stage_enrich"]
```

```
1→"""Enrichment and cluster-level validation helpers for triage stages."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→
    10→from ..helpers import cluster_issue_ids
    11→
    12→_PATH_RE = re.compile(r"(?:[\w./-]+/)?(?:src|supabase)/[\w./-]+\.\w+")
    13→_EXT_SWAPS = {".ts": ".tsx", ".tsx": ".ts", ".js": ".jsx", ".jsx": ".js"}
    14→_VALID_EFFORTS = {"trivial", "small", "medium", "large"}
    15→
    16→
    17→def _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: dict):
    18→    """Yield manual clusters that still carry issue IDs."""
    19→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    20→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
    21→            continue
    22→        yield name, cluster
    23→
    24→
    25→def _cluster_steps(cluster: dict) -> list[dict]:
    26→    """Return structured action steps for one cluster."""
    27→    return [
    28→        step
    29→        for step in (cluster.get("action_steps") or [])
    30→        if isinstance(step, dict)
    31→    ]
    32→
    33→
    34→def _detail_paths(detail: str) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Extract file paths from one step detail string."""
    36→    if not detail:
    37→        return []
    38→    return _PATH_RE.findall(detail)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _path_exists_or_alt_exists(repo_root: Path, path_str: str) -> bool:
    42→    """Return whether the referenced path or a ts/js sibling exists."""
    43→    path = repo_root / path_str
    44→    if path.exists():
    45→        return True
    46→    alt_ext = _EXT_SWAPS.get(path.suffix)
    47→    return bool(alt_ext and path.with_suffix(alt_ext).exists())
    48→
    49→
    50→def _require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages: dict) -> bool:
    51→    """Gate: organize must be done before enrich."""
    52→    from .core import require_stage_prerequisite  # noqa: PLC0415
    53→
    54→    return require_stage_prerequisite(
    55→        stages,
    56→        flow="enrich",
    57→        messages={
    58→            "observe": (
    59→                "  Cannot enrich: observe stage not complete.",
    60→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."',
    61→            ),
    62→            "reflect": (
    63→                "  Cannot enrich: reflect stage not complete.",
    64→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."',
    65→            ),
    66→            "organize": (
    67→                "  Cannot enrich: organize stage not complete.",
    68→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "..."',
    69→            ),
    70→        },
    71→    )
    72→
    73→
    74→def _underspecified_steps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
    75→    """Return (cluster_name, bare_count, total_count) for steps missing detail or issue_refs."""
    76→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
    77→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
    78→        steps = _cluster_steps(cluster)
    79→        if not steps:
    80→            continue
    81→        bare = sum(
    82→            1
    83→            for step in steps
    84→            if isinstance(step, dict) and (not step.get("detail") or not step.get("issue_refs"))
    85→        )
    86→        if bare > 0:
    87→            results.append((name, bare, len(steps)))
    88→    return results
    89→
    90→
    91→def _steps_with_bad_paths(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
    92→    """Return steps referencing file paths that don't exist on disk."""
    93→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
    94→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
    95→        for i, step in enumerate(_cluster_steps(cluster)):
    96→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
    97→            if not detail:
    98→                continue
    99→            bad = [
   100→                path_str
   101→                for path_str in _detail_paths(detail)
   102→                if not _path_exists_or_alt_exists(repo_root, path_str)
   103→            ]
   104→            if bad:
   105→                results.append((name, i + 1, bad))
   106→    return results
   107→
   108→
   109→def _steps_without_effort(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   110→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without effort tags."""
   111→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   112→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   113→        steps = _cluster_steps(cluster)
   114→        if not steps:
   115→            continue
   116→        missing = sum(
   117→            1 for step in steps if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("effort") not in _VALID_EFFORTS
   118→        )
   119→        if missing:
   120→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   121→    return results
   122→
   123→
   124→def _cluster_file_overlaps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]]:
   125→    """Return pairs of clusters with overlapping file references in step details."""
   126→    cluster_files: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   127→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   128→        paths: set[str] = set()
   129→        for step in _cluster_steps(cluster):
   130→            paths.update(_detail_paths(step.get("detail", "")))
   131→        if paths:
   132→            cluster_files[name] = paths
   133→
   134→    overlaps: list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]] = []
   135→    names = sorted(cluster_files.keys())
   136→    for i, left in enumerate(names):
   137→        for right in names[i + 1 :]:
   138→            shared = cluster_files[left] & cluster_files[right]
   139→            if shared:
   140→                overlaps.append((left, right, sorted(shared)))
   141→    return overlaps
   142→
   143→
   144→def _clusters_with_directory_scatter(
   145→    plan: dict,
   146→    *,
   147→    threshold: int = 5,
   148→) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   149→    """Return clusters whose issues span too many unrelated directories."""
   150→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   151→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   152→        dirs: set[str] = set()
   153→        for step in _cluster_steps(cluster):
   154→            for path_str in _detail_paths(step.get("detail", "")):
   155→                parts = path_str.split("/")
   156→                if len(parts) >= 3:
   157→                    dirs.add("/".join(parts[:3]))
   158→                elif len(parts) >= 2:
   159→                    dirs.add("/".join(parts[:2]))
   160→        if len(dirs) >= threshold:
   161→            results.append((name, len(dirs), sorted(dirs)[:6]))
   162→    return results
   163→
   164→
   165→def _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(
   166→    plan: dict,
   167→    *,
   168→    max_ratio: float = 1.0,
   169→) -> list[tuple[str, int, int, float]]:
   170→    """Return clusters where step count >= issue count (1:1 mapping)."""
   171→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int, float]] = []
   172→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   173→        steps = len(_cluster_steps(cluster))
   174→        issues = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   175→        if issues >= 3 and steps > 0:
   176→            ratio = steps / issues
   177→            if ratio > max_ratio:
   178→                results.append((name, steps, issues, ratio))
   179→    return results
   180→
   181→
   182→def _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   183→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without issue_refs."""
   184→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   185→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   186→        steps = _cluster_steps(cluster)
   187→        if not steps:
   188→            continue
   189→        missing = sum(1 for step in steps if not step.get("issue_refs"))
   190→        if missing:
   191→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   192→    return results
   193→
   194→
   195→def _steps_with_vague_detail(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
   196→    """Return steps with detail too vague to be executor-ready."""
   197→    del repo_root
   198→    results: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
   199→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   200→        for i, step in enumerate(_cluster_steps(cluster)):
   201→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   202→            if not detail:
   203→                results.append((name, i + 1, step.get("title", "(no title)")))
   204→                continue
   205→            has_path = bool(_detail_paths(detail))
   206→            if len(detail) < 80 and not has_path:
   207→                results.append((name, i + 1, step.get("title", "(no title)")))
   208→    return results
   209→
   210→
   211→def _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   212→    """Return steps whose issue_refs include wontfixed/skipped issues."""
   213→    wontfixed = set()
   214→    for fid, issue in plan.get("issues", {}).items():
   215→        if isinstance(issue, dict) and issue.get("status") in ("wontfix", "skipped"):
   216→            wontfixed.add(fid)
   217→    for fid in plan.get("wontfix", {}):
   218→        wontfixed.add(fid)
   219→
   220→    if not wontfixed:
   221→        return []
   222→
   223→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   224→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   225→        for i, step in enumerate(_cluster_steps(cluster)):
   226→            refs = step.get("issue_refs") or []
   227→            stale = [ref for ref in refs if ref in wontfixed]
   228→            if stale:
   229→                results.append((name, i + 1, stale))
   230→    return results
   231→
   232→
   233→def _enrich_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   234→    if not report:
   235→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage enrich.", "red"))
   236→        print(colorize("  Summarize the enrichment work you did:", "dim"))
   237→        print(colorize("  - Which clusters did you add detail/refs to?", "dim"))
   238→        print(colorize("  - Are steps specific enough for an executor with zero context?", "dim"))
   239→        print(colorize("  - Did you link issue_refs so steps auto-complete on resolve?", "dim"))
   240→        return None
   241→    if len(report) < 100:
   242→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   243→        print(colorize("  Explain what enrichment you did and why steps are executor-ready.", "dim"))
   244→        return None
   245→    return report
   246→
   247→
   248→__all__ = [
   249→    "_cluster_file_overlaps",
   250→    "_clusters_with_directory_scatter",
   251→    "_clusters_with_high_step_ratio",
   252→    "_enrich_report_or_error",
   253→    "_require_organize_stage_for_enrich",
   254→    "_steps_missing_issue_refs",
   255→    "_steps_referencing_skipped_issues",
   256→    "_steps_with_bad_paths",
   257→    "_steps_with_vague_detail",
   258→    "_steps_without_effort",
   259→    "_underspecified_steps",
   260→]
   261→
```

```
1→"""Shared enrich/sense-check quality evaluation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Literal
     8→
     9→from .enrich_checks import (
    10→    _steps_missing_issue_refs,
    11→    _steps_referencing_skipped_issues,
    12→    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    13→    _steps_with_vague_detail,
    14→    _steps_without_effort,
    15→    _underspecified_steps,
    16→)
    17→
    18→Severity = Literal["failure", "warning"]
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class EnrichQualityIssue:
    23→    """Structured enrich-quality issue with severity and source rows."""
    24→
    25→    code: str
    26→    total: int
    27→    rows: list[tuple]
    28→    message: str
    29→    severity: Severity
    30→
    31→
    32→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    33→class EnrichQualityReport:
    34→    """Structured enrich-quality result for stage flows, confirmations, and runners."""
    35→
    36→    failures: list[EnrichQualityIssue]
    37→    warnings: list[EnrichQualityIssue]
    38→
    39→    def failure(self, code: str) -> EnrichQualityIssue | None:
    40→        for issue in self.failures:
    41→            if issue.code == code:
    42→                return issue
    43→        return None
    44→
    45→    def warning(self, code: str) -> EnrichQualityIssue | None:
    46→        for issue in self.warnings:
    47→            if issue.code == code:
    48→                return issue
    49→        return None
    50→
    51→
    52→def _append_issue(
    53→    issues: list[EnrichQualityIssue],
    54→    *,
    55→    code: str,
    56→    total: int,
    57→    rows: list[tuple],
    58→    message: str,
    59→    severity: Severity,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    if total <= 0:
    62→        return
    63→    issues.append(
    64→        EnrichQualityIssue(
    65→            code=code,
    66→            total=total,
    67→            rows=rows,
    68→            message=message,
    69→            severity=severity,
    70→        )
    71→    )
    72→
    73→
    74→def evaluate_enrich_quality(
    75→    plan: dict,
    76→    repo_root: Path,
    77→    *,
    78→    phase_label: str,
    79→    bad_paths_severity: Severity,
    80→    missing_effort_severity: Severity,
    81→    include_missing_issue_refs: bool,
    82→    include_vague_detail: bool,
    83→    stale_issue_refs_severity: Severity | None,
    84→) -> EnrichQualityReport:
    85→    """Evaluate executor-readiness checks for enrich-level stage data."""
    86→    failures: list[EnrichQualityIssue] = []
    87→    warnings: list[EnrichQualityIssue] = []
    88→    sink = {"failure": failures, "warning": warnings}
    89→    sense_suffix = f" after {phase_label}" if phase_label == "sense-check" else ""
    90→
    91→    underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
    92→    _append_issue(
    93→        sink["failure"],
    94→        code="underspecified",
    95→        total=sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec),
    96→        rows=underspec,
    97→        message=f"{sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs{sense_suffix}.",
    98→        severity="failure",
    99→    )
   100→
   101→    bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
   102→    total_bad_paths = sum(len(paths) for _, _, paths in bad_paths)
   103→    bad_paths_message = (
   104→        f"{total_bad_paths} file path(s) don't exist on disk{sense_suffix}."
   105→        if phase_label == "sense-check"
   106→        else f"{total_bad_paths} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk."
   107→    )
   108→    _append_issue(
   109→        sink[bad_paths_severity],
   110→        code="bad_paths",
   111→        total=total_bad_paths,
   112→        rows=bad_paths,
   113→        message=bad_paths_message,
   114→        severity=bad_paths_severity,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    missing_effort = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   118→    total_missing_effort = sum(n for _, n, _ in missing_effort)
   119→    missing_effort_message = (
   120→        f"{total_missing_effort} step(s) have no effort tag{sense_suffix}."
   121→        if phase_label == "sense-check"
   122→        else f"{total_missing_effort} step(s) have no effort tag (trivial/small/medium/large)."
   123→    )
   124→    _append_issue(
   125→        sink[missing_effort_severity],
   126→        code="missing_effort",
   127→        total=total_missing_effort,
   128→        rows=missing_effort,
   129→        message=missing_effort_message,
   130→        severity=missing_effort_severity,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    if include_missing_issue_refs:
   134→        missing_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   135→        total_missing_refs = sum(n for _, n, _ in missing_refs)
   136→        missing_refs_message = (
   137→            f"{total_missing_refs} step(s) have no issue_refs{sense_suffix}."
   138→            if phase_label == "sense-check"
   139→            else f"{total_missing_refs} step(s) have no issue_refs for traceability."
   140→        )
   141→        _append_issue(
   142→            sink["failure"],
   143→            code="missing_issue_refs",
   144→            total=total_missing_refs,
   145→            rows=missing_refs,
   146→            message=missing_refs_message,
   147→            severity="failure",
   148→        )
   149→
   150→    if include_vague_detail:
   151→        vague_detail = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
   152→        vague_message = (
   153→            f"{len(vague_detail)} step(s) have vague detail{sense_suffix}."
   154→            if phase_label == "sense-check"
   155→            else (
   156→                f"{len(vague_detail)} step(s) have vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths). "
   157→                "Executor-ready means: file path + specific instruction."
   158→            )
   159→        )
   160→        _append_issue(
   161→            sink["failure"],
   162→            code="vague_detail",
   163→            total=len(vague_detail),
   164→            rows=vague_detail,
   165→            message=vague_message,
   166→            severity="failure",
   167→        )
   168→
   169→    if stale_issue_refs_severity is not None:
   170→        stale_refs = _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan)
   171→        total_stale_refs = sum(len(refs) for _, _, refs in stale_refs)
   172→        _append_issue(
   173→            sink[stale_issue_refs_severity],
   174→            code="stale_issue_refs",
   175→            total=total_stale_refs,
   176→            rows=stale_refs,
   177→            message=(
   178→                f"{total_stale_refs} step issue_ref(s) point to skipped/wontfixed issues"
   179→                f"{sense_suffix}. Remove stale refs."
   180→            ),
   181→            severity=stale_issue_refs_severity,
   182→        )
   183→
   184→    return EnrichQualityReport(failures=failures, warnings=warnings)
   185→
   186→
   187→__all__ = ["EnrichQualityIssue", "EnrichQualityReport", "evaluate_enrich_quality"]
   188→
```
