    62→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    63→class AutoConfirmStageRequest:
    64→    """Configuration for one fold-confirm stage auto-confirmation."""
    65→
    66→    stage_name: str
    67→    stage_label: str
    68→    blocked_heading: str
    69→    confirm_cmd: str
    70→    inline_hint: str
    71→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    72→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None
    73→
    74→
    75→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    76→class StagePrerequisite:
    77→    """One required upstream stage for a triage workflow seam."""
    78→
    79→    stage_name: str
    80→    require_confirmation: bool = False
    81→
    82→
    83→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    84→class ReflectAutoConfirmDeps:
    85→    triage_input: object | None = None
    86→    command_runtime_fn: object | None = None
    87→    collect_triage_input_fn: object = collect_triage_input
    88→    detect_recurring_patterns_fn: object = detect_recurring_patterns
    89→    save_plan_fn: object | None = None
    90→
    91→
    92→_STAGE_PREREQUISITES = {
    93→    "organize": (
    94→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    95→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
    96→    ),
    97→    "enrich": (
    98→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    99→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
   100→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
   101→    ),
   102→    "sense-check": (
   103→        StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True),
   104→    ),
   105→    "complete:organize": (
   106→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
   107→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
   108→    ),
   109→    "complete:enrich": (
   110→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
   111→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
   112→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
   113→    ),
   114→    "complete:sense-check": (
   115→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
   116→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
   117→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
   118→        StagePrerequisite("sense-check"),
   119→    ),
   120→}
   121→
   122→
   123→def _missing_stage_prerequisite(
   124→    stages: dict,
   125→    *,
   126→    flow: str,
   127→) -> StagePrerequisite | None:
   128→    """Return the first missing upstream stage required by one triage flow."""
   129→    for prerequisite in _STAGE_PREREQUISITES.get(flow, ()):
   130→        stage_record = stages.get(prerequisite.stage_name)
   131→        if stage_record is None:
   132→            return prerequisite
   133→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
   134→            return prerequisite
   135→    return None
   136→
   137→
   138→def require_stage_prerequisite(
   139→    stages: dict,
   140→    *,
   141→    flow: str,
   142→    messages: dict[str, tuple[str, str]],
   143→) -> bool:
   144→    """Print consistent prerequisite guidance for simple triage stage gates."""
   145→    missing = _missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow=flow)
   146→    if missing is None:
   147→        return True
   148→    blocked_heading, command_hint = messages[missing.stage_name]
   149→    print(colorize(blocked_heading, "red"))
   150→    print(colorize(command_hint, "dim"))
   151→    return False
   152→
   153→
   154→def _auto_confirm_stage(
   155→    *,
   156→    plan: dict,
   157→    stage_record: dict,
   158→    attestation: str | None,
   159→    request: AutoConfirmStageRequest,
   160→    save_plan_fn=None,
   161→) -> bool:
   162→    """Shared auto-confirm flow for stage fold-confirm operations."""
   163→    if save_plan_fn is None:
   164→        save_plan_fn = save_plan
   165→    if stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
   166→        return True
   167→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   168→        print(colorize(f"  {request.blocked_heading}", "red"))
   169→        print(colorize(f"  Run: {request.confirm_cmd}", "dim"))
   170→        print(colorize(f"  {request.inline_hint}", "dim"))
   171→        return False
   172→
   173→    confirmed_text = attestation.strip()
   174→    validation_err = validate_attestation(
   175→        confirmed_text,
   176→        request.stage_name,
   177→        dimensions=request.dimensions,
   178→        cluster_names=request.cluster_names,
   179→    )
   180→    if validation_err:
   181→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   182→        return False
   183→
   184→    stage_record["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   185→    stage_record["confirmed_text"] = confirmed_text
   186→    save_plan_fn(plan)
   187→    print(colorize(f"  ✓ {request.stage_label} auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   188→    return True
   189→
   190→
   191→def _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   192→    *,
   193→    plan: dict,
   194→    stages: dict,
   195→    attestation: str | None,
   196→    triage_input,
   197→    save_plan_fn=None,
   198→) -> bool:
   199→    observe_stage = stages.get("observe")
   200→    if observe_stage is None:
   201→        return False
   202→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   203→    return _auto_confirm_stage(
   204→        plan=plan,
   205→        stage_record=observe_stage,
   206→        attestation=attestation,
   207→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   208→            stage_name="observe",
   209→            stage_label="Observe",
   210→            blocked_heading="Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.",
   211→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm observe",
   212→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.",
   213→            dimensions=dim_names,
   214→        ),
   215→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   216→    )
   217→
   218→
   219→def _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
   220→    *,
   221→    report: str,
   222→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   223→    recurring: dict,
   224→) -> bool:
   225→    if not recurring_dims:
   226→        return True
   227→    report_lower = report.lower()
   228→    mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
   229→    if mentioned:
   230→        return True
   231→    print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
   232→    for dim in recurring_dims:
   233→        info = recurring[dim]
   234→        print(
   235→            colorize(
   236→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
   237→                f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
   238→                "yellow",
   239→            )
   240→        )
   241→    print(colorize("  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.", "dim"))
   242→    return False
   243→
   244→
   245→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246→# Disposition types
   247→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248→
   249→DecisionKind = Literal["cluster", "permanent_skip"]
   250→
   251→
   252→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   253→class ReflectDisposition:
   254→    """One issue's intended disposition as declared by the reflect stage."""
   255→
   256→    issue_id: str
   257→    decision: DecisionKind
   258→    target: str  # cluster name or skip reason tag
   259→
   260→    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
   261→        """Serialize for JSON persistence in plan.json."""
   262→        return {"issue_id": self.issue_id, "decision": self.decision, "target": self.target}
   263→
   264→    @classmethod
   265→    def from_dict(cls, d: dict | ReflectDisposition) -> ReflectDisposition:
   266→        """Deserialize from persisted plan.json dict, or pass through if already typed."""
   267→        if isinstance(d, cls):
   268→            return d
   269→        return cls(
   270→            issue_id=d.get("issue_id", ""),
   271→            decision=d.get("decision", "cluster"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   272→            target=d.get("target", ""),
   273→        )
   274→
   275→
   276→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   277→class ActualDisposition:
   278→    """What actually happened to an issue in plan state."""
   279→
   280→    kind: Literal["clustered", "skipped", "unplaced"]
   281→    cluster_name: str = ""  # non-empty when kind == "clustered"
   282→
   283→    def describe(self, intended: ReflectDisposition | None = None) -> str:
   284→        """Human-readable description for error messages."""
   285→        if self.kind == "skipped":
   286→            return "permanently skipped"
   287→        if self.kind == "clustered":
   288→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster" and self.cluster_name != intended.target:
   289→                return f'in cluster "{self.cluster_name}" (expected "{intended.target}")'
   290→            return f'clustered in "{self.cluster_name}"'
   291→        if self.kind == "unplaced":
   292→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster":
   293→                return "not in any cluster"
   294→            return "not skipped, not clustered"
   295→        return "unknown state"
   296→
   297→
   298→def _build_actual_disposition_index(plan: dict) -> dict[str, ActualDisposition]:
   299→    """Build a lookup from issue ID to its actual disposition in plan state."""
   300→    index: dict[str, ActualDisposition] = {}
   301→
   302→    for cluster_name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   303→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   304→            continue
   305→        for fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   306→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="clustered", cluster_name=cluster_name)
   307→
   308→    for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}):
   309→        if isinstance(fid, str):
   310→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="skipped")
   311→
   312→    return index
   313→
   314→
   315→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   316→# Coverage Ledger parsing — shared infrastructure
   317→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→
   319→def _build_id_resolution_maps(
   320→    valid_ids: set[str],
   321→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, str]]:
   322→    """Build short-ID lookup structures from a set of full issue IDs."""
   323→    short_id_buckets: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   324→    short_hex_map: dict[str, str] = {}
   325→    for issue_id in sorted(valid_ids):
   326→        short_id = issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   327→        short_id_buckets.setdefault(short_id, []).append(issue_id)
   328→        if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", short_id):
   329→            existing = short_hex_map.get(short_id)
   330→            if existing is None:
   331→                short_hex_map[short_id] = issue_id
   332→            elif existing != issue_id:
   333→                short_hex_map.pop(short_id, None)
   334→    return short_id_buckets, short_hex_map
   335→
   336→
   337→def _clean_ledger_token(raw: str) -> str:
   338→    """Strip backticks, brackets, and whitespace from a ledger token."""
   339→    token = raw.strip().strip("`").strip()
   340→    if token.startswith("[") and token.endswith("]"):
   341→        token = token[1:-1].strip()
   342→    return token
   343→
   344→
   345→def _extract_ledger_entry(line: str) -> tuple[str, str | None, str | None]:
   346→    """Parse a ledger line into (token, decision, target).
   347→
   348→    Always extracts the token (issue key) when a ``->`` is present.
   349→    Decision and target are None when the right-hand side doesn't match
   350→    the ``decision "target"`` pattern (e.g. ``-> TODO``).
   351→    Returns ``("", None, None)`` when the line has no ``->``.
   352→    """
   353→    # Try full structured form: - token -> decision "target"
   354→    match = re.match(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(\w+)\s+[\"']([^\"']+)[\"']", line)
   355→    if match:
   356→        [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   357→        return token, match.group(2).strip().lower(), match.group(3).strip()
   358→
   359→    # Try unquoted: - token -> decision target-slug
   360→    match = re.match(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(\w+)\s+(\S+.*)", line)
   361→    if match:
   362→        [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   363→        target = match.group(3).strip().strip("\"'")
   364→        return token, match.group(2).strip().lower(), target
   365→
   366→    # Bare arrow: - token -> (anything or nothing)
   367→    match = re.match(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->", line)
   368→    if match:
   369→        [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   370→        return token, None, None
   371→
   372→    return "", None, None
   373→
   374→
   375→def _resolve_token_to_id(
   376→    token: str,
   377→    valid_ids: set[str],
   378→    short_id_buckets: dict[str, list[str]],
   379→    short_hex_map: dict[str, str],
   380→    short_id_usage: Counter[str],
   381→) -> str | None:
   382→    """Resolve a ledger token to a full issue ID, or None."""
   383→    if token in valid_ids:
   384→        return token
   385→    bucket = short_id_buckets.get(token)
   386→    if bucket:
   387→        bucket_index = short_id_usage[token]
   388→        issue_id = bucket[bucket_index] if bucket_index < len(bucket) else bucket[-1]
   389→        short_id_usage[token] += 1
   390→        return issue_id
   391→    # Hex-substring fallback
   392→    for hex_token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", token):
   393→        resolved = short_hex_map.get(hex_token)
   394→        if resolved:
   395→            return resolved
   396→    return None
   397→
   398→
   399→# Canonical decision values for dispositions
   400→_CLUSTER_DECISIONS = frozenset({"cluster"})
   401→_SKIP_DECISIONS = frozenset({"skip", "dismiss", "defer", "drop", "remove"})
   402→
   403→
   404→def _normalize_decision(raw: str) -> str:
   405→    """Normalize a ledger decision to 'cluster' or 'permanent_skip'."""
   406→    lower = raw.lower()
   407→    if lower in _CLUSTER_DECISIONS:
   408→        return "cluster"
   409→    if lower in _SKIP_DECISIONS:
   410→        return "permanent_skip"
   411→    return lower
   412→
   413→
   414→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   415→# Single-pass ledger walker — produces both accounting and dispositions
   416→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   417→
   418→@dataclass
   419→class _LedgerParseResult:
   420→    """Combined output of a single pass over the Coverage Ledger section."""
   421→
   422→    hits: Counter[str]  # issue_id -> mention count (for accounting)
   423→    dispositions: list[ReflectDisposition]  # structured dispositions
   424→    found_section: bool  # True if ## Coverage Ledger was present
   425→
   426→
   427→def _walk_coverage_ledger(
   428→    report: str,
   429→    valid_ids: set[str],
   430→) -> _LedgerParseResult:
   431→    """Single-pass parse of the Coverage Ledger section.
   432→
   433→    Extracts both hit counts (for accounting validation) and structured
   434→    dispositions (for the execution contract) in one walk.
   435→    """
   436→    short_id_buckets, short_hex_map = _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids)
   437→    hits: Counter[str] = Counter()
   438→    dispositions: list[dict] = []
   439→    short_id_usage: Counter[str] = Counter()
   440→    in_ledger = False
   441→    found_section = False
   442→
   443→    for raw_line in report.splitlines():
   444→        line = raw_line.strip()
   445→        if re.fullmatch(r"##\s+Coverage Ledger", line, re.IGNORECASE):
   446→            in_ledger = True
   447→            found_section = True
   448→            continue
   449→        if in_ledger and re.match(r"##\s+", line):
   450→            break
   451→        if not in_ledger:
   452→            continue
   453→
   454→        token, decision, target = _extract_ledger_entry(line)
   455→        if not token:
   456→            continue
   457→
   458→        issue_id = _resolve_token_to_id(
   459→            token, valid_ids, short_id_buckets, short_hex_map, short_id_usage,
   460→        )
   461→        if not issue_id:
   462→            # Last resort: scan entire line for hex IDs
   463→            for hex_token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", line):
   464→                resolved = short_hex_map.get(hex_token)
   465→                if resolved:
   466→                    issue_id = resolved
   467→                    break
   468→
   469→        if issue_id:
   470→            hits[issue_id] += 1
   471→            if decision and target:
   472→                normalized = _normalize_decision(decision)
   473→                if normalized in ("cluster", "permanent_skip"):
   474→                    dispositions.append(ReflectDisposition(
   475→                        issue_id=issue_id,
   476→                        decision=normalized,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   477→                        target=target,
   478→                    ))
   479→
   480→    return _LedgerParseResult(
   481→        hits=hits,
   482→        dispositions=dispositions,
   483→        found_section=found_section,
   484→    )
   485→
   486→
   487→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   488→# Public API — thin wrappers over the shared walker
   489→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   490→
   491→def parse_reflect_dispositions(
   492→    report: str,
   493→    valid_ids: set[str],
   494→) -> list[ReflectDisposition]:
   495→    """Parse the Coverage Ledger into structured dispositions.
   496→
   497→    Returns an empty list if no Coverage Ledger section is found.
   498→    """
   499→    return _walk_coverage_ledger(report, valid_ids).dispositions
   500→
   501→
   502→def _analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   503→    *,
   504→    report: str,
   505→    valid_ids: set[str],
   506→) -> tuple[set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   507→    """Return cited, missing, and duplicate issue IDs referenced by reflect."""
   508→    result = _walk_coverage_ledger(report, valid_ids)
   509→
   510→    if result.found_section and result.hits:
   511→        cited = set(result.hits)
   512→        duplicates = sorted(
   513→            issue_id for issue_id, count in result.hits.items() if count > 1
   514→        )
   515→        missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   516→        return cited, missing, duplicates
   517→
   518→    # Fallback for reports without a Coverage Ledger section
   519→    short_id_buckets, short_hex_map = _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids)
   520→    cited = extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
   521→    for issue_id in valid_ids:
   522→        if issue_id in report:
   523→            cited.add(issue_id)
   524→    short_hits = Counter(
   525→        short_hex_map[token]
   526→        for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", report)
   527→        if token in short_hex_map
   528→    )
   529→    duplicates = sorted(
   530→        issue_id for issue_id, count in short_hits.items() if count > 1
   531→    )
   532→    missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   533→    return cited, missing, duplicates
   534→
   535→
   536→def _validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   537→    *,
   538→    report: str,
   539→    valid_ids: set[str],
   540→) -> tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   541→    """Ensure the reflect blueprint accounts for every open review issue exactly once."""
   542→    cited, missing, duplicates = _analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   543→        report=report,
   544→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   545→    )
   546→    if not missing and not duplicates:
   547→        return True, cited, missing, duplicates
   548→    print(
   549→        colorize(
   550→            "  Reflect report must account for every open review issue exactly once.",
   551→            "red",
   552→        )
   553→    )
   554→    if missing:
   555→        missing_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in missing[:10])
   556→        print(colorize(f"    Missing: {missing_short}", "yellow"))
   557→    if duplicates:
   558→        duplicate_short = ", ".join(
   559→            issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in duplicates[:10]
   560→        )
   561→        print(colorize(f"    Duplicated: {duplicate_short}", "yellow"))
   562→    print(colorize("  Fix the reflect blueprint before running organize.", "dim"))
   563→    return False, cited, missing, duplicates
   564→
   565→
   566→def _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages: dict) -> bool:
   567→    return require_stage_prerequisite(
   568→        stages,
   569→        flow="organize",
   570→        messages={
   571→            "observe": (
   572→                "  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.",
   573→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."',
   574→            ),
   575→            "reflect": (
   576→                "  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.",
   577→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."',
   578→            ),
   579→        },
   580→    )
   581→
   582→
   583→def _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   584→    *,
   585→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   586→    plan: dict,
   587→    stages: dict,
   588→    attestation: str | None,
   589→    deps: ReflectAutoConfirmDeps | None = None,
   590→) -> bool:
   591→    reflect_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   592→    if reflect_stage is None:
   593→        return False
   594→
   595→    deps = deps or ReflectAutoConfirmDeps()
   596→    resolved_triage_input = deps.triage_input
   597→    if resolved_triage_input is None:
   598→        runtime_factory = deps.command_runtime_fn or command_runtime
   599→        runtime = runtime_factory(args)
   600→        resolved_triage_input = deps.collect_triage_input_fn(plan, runtime.state)
   601→
   602→    valid_ids = set(resolved_triage_input.open_issues.keys())
   603→    accounting_ok, cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = _validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   604→        report=str(reflect_stage.get("report", "")),
   605→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   606→    )
   607→    if not accounting_ok:
   608→        return False
   609→    reflect_stage["cited_ids"] = sorted(cited_ids)
   610→    reflect_stage["missing_issue_ids"] = missing_ids
   611→    reflect_stage["duplicate_issue_ids"] = duplicate_ids
   612→
   613→    recurring = deps.detect_recurring_patterns_fn(
   614→        resolved_triage_input.open_issues,
   615→        resolved_triage_input.resolved_issues,
   616→    )
   617→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(resolved_triage_input)
   618→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   619→    reflect_clusters = [name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")]
   620→    return _auto_confirm_stage(
   621→        plan=plan,
   622→        stage_record=reflect_stage,
   623→        attestation=attestation,
   624→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   625→            stage_name="reflect",
   626→            stage_label="Reflect",
   627→            blocked_heading="Cannot organize: reflect stage not confirmed.",
   628→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect",
   629→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm reflect inline.",
   630→            dimensions=reflect_dims,
   631→            cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   632→        ),
   633→        save_plan_fn=deps.save_plan_fn,
   634→    )
   635→
   636→
   637→def _manual_clusters_or_error(
   638→    plan: dict,
   639→    *,
   640→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   641→) -> list[str] | None:
   642→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   643→    if manual_clusters:
   644→        return manual_clusters
   645→    if open_review_ids is not None and not open_review_ids:
   646→        return []
   647→    any_clusters = [
   648→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   649→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
   650→    ]
   651→    if any_clusters:
   652→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
   653→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
   654→    else:
   655→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   656→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   657→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
   658→    return None
   659→
   660→
   661→def _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan: dict) -> bool:
   662→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   663→    if not gaps:
   664→        return True
   665→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.", "red"))
   666→    for name, missing in gaps:
   667→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   668→    print()
   669→    print(colorize("  Each cluster needs a description and action steps:", "dim"))
   670→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "what this cluster addresses" --steps "step 1" "step 2"', "dim"))
   671→    return False
   672→
   673→
   674→def _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   675→    """Block if open review issues aren't in any manual cluster. Return True if OK."""
   676→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   677→    if not unclustered:
   678→        return True
   679→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no cluster.", "red"))
   680→    for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   681→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   682→        print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   683→    if len(unclustered) > 10:
   684→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   685→    print()
   686→    print(colorize("  Every review issue needs an action plan. Either:", "dim"))
   687→    print(colorize("    1. Add to a cluster: desloppify plan cluster add <name> <pattern>", "dim"))
   688→    print(colorize('    2. Wontfix it: desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "reason" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   689→    return False
   690→
   691→
   692→def _organize_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   693→    if not report:
   694→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   695→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   696→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory issues before clustering?", "dim"))
   697→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   698→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   699→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   700→        return None
   701→    if len(report) < 100:
   702→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   703→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   704→        return None
   705→    return report
   706→
   707→
   708→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   709→class LedgerMismatch:
   710→    """One issue where plan state diverges from the reflect disposition."""
   711→
   712→    intended: ReflectDisposition
   713→    actual: ActualDisposition
   714→
   715→    @property
   716→    def issue_id(self) -> str:
   717→        return self.intended.issue_id
   718→
   719→    @property
   720→    def expected_decision(self) -> str:
   721→        return self.intended.decision
   722→
   723→    @property
   724→    def expected_target(self) -> str:
   725→        return self.intended.target
   726→
   727→    @property
   728→    def actual_state(self) -> str:
   729→        return self.actual.describe(self.intended)
   730→
   731→
   732→def _disposition_matches(intended: ReflectDisposition, actual: ActualDisposition) -> bool:
   733→    """True when the actual plan state satisfies the intended disposition."""
   734→    if intended.decision == "permanent_skip":
   735→        return actual.kind == "skipped"
   736→    if intended.decision == "cluster":
   737→        return actual.kind == "clustered" and actual.cluster_name == intended.target
   738→    return False
   739→
   740→
   741→def validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   742→    *,
   743→    plan: dict,
   744→    stages: dict,
   745→) -> list[LedgerMismatch]:
   746→    """Check that plan mutations match the reflect disposition ledger.
   747→
   748→    Returns an empty list when all dispositions are faithfully materialized,
   749→    or a list of mismatches when organize diverged from the reflect plan.
   750→
   751→    Silently returns [] for legacy runs without a disposition_ledger.
   752→    """
   753→    raw_ledger = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("disposition_ledger")
   754→    if not raw_ledger:
   755→        return []
   756→
   757→    ledger = [ReflectDisposition.from_dict(d) for d in raw_ledger]
   758→    actuals = _build_actual_disposition_index(plan)
   759→    unplaced = ActualDisposition(kind="unplaced")
   760→
   761→    return [
   762→        LedgerMismatch(intended=d, actual=actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   763→        for d in ledger
   764→        if not _disposition_matches(d, actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   765→    ]
   766→
   767→
   768→def _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   769→    *,
   770→    plan: dict,
   771→    stages: dict,
   772→) -> bool:
   773→    """Block organize if plan state diverges from the reflect disposition ledger."""
   774→    mismatches = validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   775→        plan=plan, stages=stages,
   776→    )
   777→    if not mismatches:
   778→        return True
   779→
   780→    print(
   781→        colorize(
   782→            f"  Cannot organize: {len(mismatches)} issue(s) diverge from the reflect plan.",
   783→            "red",
   784→        )
   785→    )
   786→    for m in mismatches[:10]:
   787→        short_id = m.issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   788→        print(
   789→            colorize(
   790→                f"    {short_id}: reflect said {m.expected_decision} "
   791→                f'"{m.expected_target}", but {m.actual_state}',
   792→                "yellow",
   793→            )
   794→        )
   795→    if len(mismatches) > 10:
   796→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(mismatches) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   797→    print()
   798→    print(
   799→        colorize(
   800→            "  Fix the plan to match the reflect ledger, or re-run reflect to update dispositions.",
   801→            "dim",
   802→        )
   803→    )
   804→    return False
   805→

1→"""Enrich stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→

    54→def _resolve_enrich_stage_context(
    55→    *,
    56→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    57→    services: TriageServices | None,
    58→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
    59→) -> tuple[TriageServices, dict, dict, dict, str | None, str | None]:
    60→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    61→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    62→    resolved_services = services or deps.default_triage_services()
    63→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    64→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
    65→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    66→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    67→    return resolved_services, plan, state, stages, report, attestation
    68→
    69→
    70→def _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
    71→    *,
    72→    plan: dict,
    73→    stages: dict,
    74→    attestation: str | None,
    75→    services: TriageServices,
    76→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
    77→) -> bool:
    78→    if stages.get("organize", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
    79→        return True
    80→    if not attestation:
    81→        print(deps.colorize("  Cannot enrich: organize stage not confirmed.", "red"))
    82→        print(deps.colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
    83→        print(deps.colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.", "dim"))
    84→        return False
    85→
    86→    auto_confirm_organize_for_complete = deps.auto_confirm_organize_for_complete
    87→    if auto_confirm_organize_for_complete is None:
    88→        from ..validation.completion_stages import _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete
    89→
    90→        auto_confirm_organize_for_complete = _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete
    91→    return auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
    92→        plan=plan,
    93→        stages=stages,
    94→        stage="organize",
    95→        attestation=attestation,
    96→        save_plan_fn=services.save_plan,
    97→    )
    98→
    99→
   100→def _require_cluster_update_activity(
   101→    *,
   102→    plan: dict,
   103→    state: dict,
   104→    stages: dict,
   105→    attestation: str | None,
   106→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   107→) -> bool:
   108→    organize_ts = stages.get("organize", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   109→    if not organize_ts:
   110→        return True
   111→    activity = deps.count_log_activity_since(plan, organize_ts)
   112→    update_ops = activity.get("cluster_update", 0)
   113→    if update_ops != 0 or not open_review_ids_from_state(state):
   114→        return True
   115→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
   116→        print(
   117→            deps.colorize(
   118→                "  Note: 0 cluster_update ops logged since organize. "
   119→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        return True
   124→    print(
   125→        deps.colorize(
   126→            "  Cannot enrich: no cluster_update operations logged since organize.",
   127→            "red",
   128→        )
   129→    )
   130→    print(
   131→        deps.colorize(
   132→            "  Enriching steps requires running cluster update commands.\n"
   133→            '  e.g. desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..."',
   134→            "dim",
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→    print(
   138→        deps.colorize(
   139→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why no update ops" (40+ chars).',
   140→            "dim",
   141→        )
   142→    )
   143→    return False
   144→
   145→
   146→def _print_underspecified_step_error(
   147→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]],
   148→    *,
   149→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   152→    print(
   153→        deps.colorize(
   154→            f"  Cannot enrich: {total_bare} step(s) across {len(underspec)} cluster(s) lack detail or issue_refs:",
   155→            "red",
   156→        )
   157→    )
   158→    for name, bare, total in underspec:
   159→        print(deps.colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   160→    print()
   161→    print(
   162→        deps.colorize(
   163→            "  Every step needs --detail (sub-points) or --issue-refs (for auto-completion).",
   164→            "dim",
   165→        )
   166→    )
   167→    print(deps.colorize("  Fix:", "dim"))
   168→    print(
   169→        deps.colorize(
   170→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
   171→            "dim",
   172→        )
   173→    )
   174→    print(
   175→        deps.colorize(
   176→            "  You can also still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder, etc.",
   177→            "dim",
   178→        )
   179→    )
   180→
   181→

   183→    *,

   186→) -> None:
   187→    bad_paths = report.warning("bad_paths")

   191→                f"  Warning: {bad_paths.total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk:",

   193→            )
   194→        )
   195→        for name, step_num, paths in bad_paths.rows[:5]:
   196→            print(deps.colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {', '.join(paths[:3])}", "yellow"))
   197→        print(
   198→            deps.colorize(
   199→                "  Fix paths before confirming enrich (confirmation will block on bad paths).",

    44→def _sense_check_report(
    45→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    46→    stages: dict,
    47→    *,
    48→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps,
    49→) -> tuple[str | None, bool, dict | None]:
    50→    existing_stage = stages.get("sense-check")

    52→    report, is_reuse = deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)

    55→
    56→def _sense_check_quality_problems(
    57→    plan: dict,
    58→    *,
    59→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps,
    60→) -> list[str]:
    61→    get_project_root = deps.get_project_root
    62→    if get_project_root is None:
    63→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    64→
    65→    repo_root = get_project_root()
    66→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
    67→        plan,
    68→        repo_root,
    69→        phase_label="sense-check",
    70→        bad_paths_severity="failure",
    71→        missing_effort_severity="failure",
    72→        include_missing_issue_refs=True,
    73→        include_vague_detail=True,
    74→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
    75→    )
    76→    return [issue.message for issue in quality_report.failures]

    78→
    79→def _print_sense_check_problems(
    80→    problems: list[str],
    81→    *,

    83→) -> None:

    85→    for problem in problems:

    89→
    90→def _report_length_ok(report: str | None, *, colorize_fn: ColorizeFn) -> bool:

    93→        print(
    94→            colorize_fn(

    96→                "dim",

    98→        )
    99→        return False
   100→    if len(report) < 100:
   101→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   102→        return False
   103→    return True

   105→
   106→def _sense_check_evidence_failures(
   107→    report: str,
   108→    *,
   109→    plan: dict,
   110→    state: dict,
   111→) -> tuple[list[object], list[object]]:
   112→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   113→        validate_report_has_file_paths,
   114→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   115→    )
   116→    from ..helpers import manual_clusters_with_issues
   117→
   118→    failures: list[object] = []
   119→    if open_review_ids_from_state(state):
   120→        failures.extend(validate_report_has_file_paths(report) or [])
   121→
   122→    cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   123→    if cluster_names:
   124→        failures.extend(validate_report_references_clusters(report, cluster_names) or [])
   125→
   126→    blocking = [failure for failure in failures if failure.blocking]
   127→    advisory = [failure for failure in failures if not failure.blocking]
   128→    return blocking, advisory

1→"""Per-stage subagent prompt builders for triage runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections import Counter
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    11→    TriageInput,
    12→    build_triage_prompt,
    13→)
    14→
    15→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    16→from .stage_prompts_instruction_blocks import _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS
    17→from .stage_prompts_instruction_shared import (
    18→    _STAGES,
    19→    PromptMode,
    20→    render_cli_reference,
    21→    triage_prompt_preamble,
    22→)
    23→from .stage_prompts_observe import (
    24→    _observe_batch_instructions,
    25→    build_observe_batch_prompt,
    26→)
    27→from .stage_prompts_sense import (
    28→    build_sense_check_content_prompt,
    29→    build_sense_check_structure_prompt,
    30→)

    33→
    34→def _required_issue_hashes(triage_input: TriageInput) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Return sorted short hashes for open review issues."""
    36→    return sorted(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in triage_input.open_issues)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _compact_issue_summary(triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    40→    """Return a compact issue summary for later triage stages."""
    41→    by_dim: Counter[str] = Counter()
    42→    for issue in triage_input.open_issues.values():
    43→        detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    44→        by_dim[str(detail.get("dimension", "unknown"))] += 1
    45→    dims = ", ".join(f"{name} ({count})" for name, count in sorted(by_dim.items()))
    46→    parts = [
    47→        "## Issue Summary",
    48→        f"Open review issues: {len(triage_input.open_issues)}",
    49→    ]
    50→    if dims:
    51→        parts.append(f"Open dimensions: {dims}")
    52→    if triage_input.new_since_last:
    53→        parts.append(f"New since last triage: {len(triage_input.new_since_last)}")
    54→    if triage_input.resolved_since_last:
    55→        parts.append(f"Resolved since last triage: {len(triage_input.resolved_since_last)}")
    56→    return "\n".join(parts)
    57→
    58→
    59→def _issue_context_for_stage(stage: str, triage_input: TriageInput) -> str:
    60→    """Return the amount of issue context appropriate for a stage."""
    61→    if stage in {"observe", "reflect"}:
    62→        parts = ["## Issue Data\n\n" + build_triage_prompt(triage_input)]
    63→        if stage == "reflect":
    64→            short_ids = _required_issue_hashes(triage_input)
    65→            parts.append(
    66→                "## Required Issue Hashes\n"
    67→                f"Total open review issues: {len(short_ids)}\n"
    68→                "Every one of these hashes must appear exactly once in your cluster/skip blueprint.\n"
    69→                "Do not repeat hashes outside that blueprint.\n"
    70→                + ", ".join(short_ids)
    71→            )
    72→            parts.append(
    73→                "## Coverage Ledger Template\n"
    74→                "Your final report MUST contain a `## Coverage Ledger` section with one line per issue.\n"
    75→                "Allowed forms:\n"
    76→                '- `- abcd1234 -> cluster "cluster-name"`\n'
    77→                '- `- abcd1234 -> skip "specific-reason-tag"`\n'
    78→                "Do not mention hashes outside the `## Coverage Ledger` section.\n"
    79→                + "\n".join(f"- {short_id} -> TODO" for short_id in short_ids)
    80→            )
    81→        return "\n\n".join(parts)
    82→    return _compact_issue_summary(triage_input)
    83→
    84→
    85→def _format_assessments_table(assessments: list[dict]) -> str:
    86→    """Format structured observe assessments as a readable table for downstream stages."""
    87→    if not assessments:
    88→        return ""
    89→    lines = ["### Structured Observe Assessments\n"]
    90→    lines.append("| Hash | Verdict | Recommendation |")
    91→    lines.append("|------|---------|----------------|")
    92→    for a in assessments:
    93→        h = a.get("hash", "?")
    94→        v = a.get("verdict", "?")
    95→        r = a.get("recommendation", "")
    96→        # Truncate recommendation for table readability
    97→        if len(r) > 80:
    98→            r = r[:77] + "..."
    99→        lines.append(f"| {h} | {v} | {r} |")
   100→    return "\n".join(lines)
   101→
   102→
   103→def _relevant_prior_reports(
   104→    stage: str,
   105→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   106→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   107→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   108→    """Return the stage reports that matter for the current stage."""
   109→    wanted = {
   110→        "reflect": ("observe",),
   111→        "organize": ("reflect",),
   112→        "enrich": ("organize",),
   113→        "sense-check": ("organize", "enrich"),
   114→    }.get(stage, tuple(prior_reports))
   115→    result = [(name, prior_reports[name]) for name in wanted if name in prior_reports]
   116→
   117→    # Append structured observe assessments for stages that need verdict data
   118→    if stage in {"reflect", "organize"} and stages_data:
   119→        observe_data = stages_data.get("observe", {})
   120→        assessments = observe_data.get("assessments", [])
   121→        if assessments:
   122→            table = _format_assessments_table(assessments)
   123→            if table:
   124→                result.append(("observe-assessments", table))
   125→
   126→    return result
   127→
   128→
   129→def build_stage_prompt(
   130→    stage: str,
   131→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   132→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   133→    *,
   134→    repo_root: Path,
   135→    mode: PromptMode = "self_record",
   136→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",
   137→    stages_data: dict | None = None,
   138→) -> str:
   139→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
   140→    parts: list[str] = []
   141→
   142→    # Preamble
   143→    parts.append(
   144→        triage_prompt_preamble(mode).format(
   145→            stage=stage.upper(),
   146→            repo_root=repo_root,
   147→            cli_command=cli_command,
   148→        )
   149→    )
   150→

1→"""Completion strategy/policy validation helpers for triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE
     6→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import extract_issue_citations
     8→
     9→from ..display.dashboard import show_plan_summary
    10→from ..helpers import (
    11→    cluster_issue_ids,
    12→    manual_clusters_with_issues,
    13→    open_review_ids_from_state,
    14→)
    15→from ..stages.helpers import unclustered_review_issues, unenriched_clusters
    16→
    17→
    18→def _completion_clusters_valid(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> bool:
    19→    if state is not None and not open_review_ids_from_state(state):
    20→        return True
    21→
    22→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    23→    if not manual_clusters:
    24→        any_clusters = [
    25→            name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
    26→            if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    27→        ]
    28→        if not any_clusters:
    29→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
    30→            print(colorize('  Create clusters: desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    31→            return False
    32→
    33→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
    34→    if gaps:
    35→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment.", "red"))
    36→        for name, missing in gaps:
    37→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    38→        print(colorize("  Small clusters (<5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue.", "dim"))
    39→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
    40→        return False
    41→
    42→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
    43→    if unclustered:
    44→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no action plan.", "red"))
    45→        for fid in unclustered[:5]:
    46→            short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
    47→            print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
    48→        if len(unclustered) > 5:
    49→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 5} more", "yellow"))
    50→        print(colorize("  Add to a cluster or wontfix each unclustered issue.", "dim"))
    51→        return False
    52→
    53→    return True
    54→
    55→
    56→def _resolve_completion_strategy(strategy: str | None, *, meta: dict) -> str | None:
    57→    if strategy:
    58→        return strategy
    59→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
    60→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
    61→    if existing:
    62→        print(colorize(f"  Current strategy: {existing}", "dim"))
    63→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
    64→    else:
    65→        print(colorize('  Provide --strategy "execution plan describing priorities, ordering, and verification approach"', "dim"))
    66→    return None
    67→
    68→
    69→def _completion_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
    70→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
    71→        return True
    72→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
    73→        return True
    74→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
    75→    print(colorize("  The strategy should describe:", "dim"))
    76→    print(colorize("    - Execution order and priorities", "dim"))
    77→    print(colorize("    - What each cluster accomplishes", "dim"))
    78→    print(colorize("    - How to verify the work is correct", "dim"))
    79→    return False
    80→
    81→
    82→def _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta: dict) -> bool:
    83→    if meta.get("strategy_summary", ""):
    84→        return True
    85→    print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no prior triage has been completed.", "red"))
    86→    print(colorize("  The full OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT flow is required the first time.", "dim"))
    87→    print(colorize(f"  Create and enrich clusters, then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
    88→    return False
    89→
    90→
    91→def _confirm_existing_stages_valid(*, stages: dict, has_only_additions: bool, si) -> bool:
    92→    if has_only_additions:
    93→        from ..stages.rendering import _print_new_issues_since_last  # noqa: PLC0415
    94→
    95→        _print_new_issues_since_last(si)
    96→        return True
    97→    if "observe" not in stages:
    98→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
    99→        print(colorize("  You must read issues first.", "dim"))
   100→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   101→        return False
   102→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   103→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   104→        print(colorize("  You must compare against completed work first.", "dim"))
   105→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   106→        return False
   107→    return True
   108→
   109→
   110→def _confirm_note_valid(note: str | None) -> bool:
   111→    if not note:
   112→        print(colorize("  --note is required for confirm-existing.", "red"))
   113→        print(colorize('  Explain why the existing plan is still valid (min 100 chars).', "dim"))
   114→        return False
   115→    if len(note) < 100:
   116→        print(colorize(f"  Note too short: {len(note)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   117→        return False
   118→    return True
   119→
   120→
   121→def _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   122→    strategy: str | None,
   123→    *,
   124→    has_only_additions: bool,
   125→    meta: dict,
   126→) -> str | None:
   127→    if strategy:
   128→        return strategy
   129→    if has_only_additions:
   130→        return "same"
   131→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   132→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   133→    if existing:
   134→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   135→    return None
   136→
   137→
   138→def _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   139→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
   140→        return True
   141→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
   142→        return True
   143→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   144→    return False
   145→
   146→
   147→def _confirmed_text_or_error(*, plan: dict, state: dict, confirmed: str | None) -> str | None:
   148→    from ..confirmations.basic import MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN  # noqa: PLC0415
   149→
   150→    if confirmed and len(confirmed.strip()) >= MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   151→        return confirmed.strip()
   152→    print(colorize("  Current plan:", "bold"))
   153→    show_plan_summary(plan, state)
   154→    if confirmed:
   155→        print(colorize(f"\n  --confirmed text too short ({len(confirmed.strip())} chars, min {MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).", "red"))
   156→    print(colorize('\n  Add --confirmed "I validate this plan..." to proceed.', "dim"))
   157→    return None
   158→
   159→
   160→def _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note: str, si) -> bool:
   161→    new_ids = si.new_since_last
   162→    if not new_ids:
   163→        return True
   164→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   165→    cited = extract_issue_citations(note, valid_ids)
   166→    new_cited = cited & new_ids
   167→    if new_cited:
   168→        return True
   169→    print(colorize("  Note must cite at least 1 new/changed issue.", "red"))
   170→    print(colorize(f"  {len(new_ids)} new issue(s) since last triage:", "dim"))
   171→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   172→        print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   173→    if len(new_ids) > 5:
   174→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   175→    return False
   176→
   177→
   178→__all__ = [

    22→def _find_referenced_names(text: str, names: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    23→    if not names:
    24→        return []
    25→    return [
    26→        name for name in names
    27→        if name.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or name.lower() in text
    28→    ]
    29→
    30→
    31→def _validate_observe_attestation(text: str, dimensions: list[str] | None) -> str | None:
    32→    found = _find_referenced_names(text, dimensions)
    33→    if found or not dimensions:
    34→        return None
    35→    dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    36→    return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    37→

    44→) -> str | None:
    45→    refs = _find_referenced_names(text, dimensions) + _find_referenced_names(text, cluster_names)
    46→    if refs or not (dimensions or cluster_names):
    47→        return None
    48→    return (
    49→        "Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    50→        f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    51→        f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    52→    )

    64→    names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    65→    return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster you {action}. Mention one of: {names}"

   100→    validator = validators.get(stage)

Perfect! I have the full context on both issues. Here's what I found:

## Issue 397: Enforce executor-ready detail standard

**Title:** "Enforce executor-ready detail standard for all queue items"

**Core Problem:** ~60% of queue items have vague action steps that require developers to re-do the analysis the tool should have already done. The issue uses `payments-auth-hardening` as the gold standard showing what executor-ready steps look like (exact file paths, line numbers, specific field names, exact changes to make).

**Requested Changes:**
1. Stricter enrich-stage validation to reject vague steps (lacking file paths/line numbers/specific identifiers)
2. Test coverage items should specify *what to test* (behaviors/edge cases), not just which file
3. Flag architecture observations separately from executable tasks
4. Use `payments-auth-hardening` as the reference standard for enforced minimum detail

---

## Issue 398: Triage process friction

**Title:** "Triage process has significant friction for AI agents (and probably humans too)"

**Context:** Triage cycle took ~45 minutes for 42 review issues. Six major friction points:

1. **Coverage Ledger format undocumented** (~15 min) — The `->` arrow format wasn't documented; validator uses `_extract_ledger_entry()`
2. **Issue ID citation format ambiguous** (~10 min) — Full IDs required, not slugs/hashes
3. **Sense-check blocks on ALL clusters** — Validates old clusters from previous cycles with stale paths
4. **Bad path errors are non-specific** — Doesn't say which path or cluster failed
5. **Five mandatory stages is heavy** — When clusters already exist, observe/reflect/organize are redundant
6. **Shell escaping on long reports** — Passing 2000+ character reports via shell is fragile

---

## Relationship Between Issues

**397 and 398 are complementary but different:**

- **397** addresses the *content quality* of queue items (enrich stage output) — making steps executor-ready with specific paths/line numbers
- **398** addresses the *process friction* of getting to that point — documentation gaps, validation ambiguities, and workflow inefficiencies in the five-stage triage pipeline

**Issue 399** just appeared (opened 2026-03-12T02:27:41Z, after 398) and is titled "Path validator fundamentally incompatible with executor-ready step descriptions" — this likely connects both, since issue 397 demands executor-ready steps with specific line numbers, but issue 398 reveals that the sense-check validation creates friction around file paths. Issue 399 probably deepens that conflict.

1→"""Per-stage instruction text blocks for triage prompts."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .stage_prompts_instruction_shared import PromptMode
     6→
     7→
     8→def _observe_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
     9→    tail = """\
    10→When done, run:
    11→```
    12→desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "<your analysis with structured assessments>"
    13→```
    14→"""
    15→    if mode == "output_only":
    16→        tail = """\
    17→When done, write a plain-text observe report with structured assessments per issue.
    18→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
    19→"""
    20→    return f"""\
    21→## OBSERVE Stage Instructions
    22→
    23→Your task: verify every open review issue against the actual source code.
    24→
    25→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
    26→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
    27→- Describe code that was already refactored
    28→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
    29→- Count props/returns/args wrong
    30→
    31→Your job is to catch these. An observe report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
    32→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
    33→
    34→Do NOT analyze themes, strategy, or relationships between issues. That's the next stage (reflect).
    35→Just verify: is each issue real?
    36→
    37→**CRITICAL: You must cite specific issue IDs (hash prefixes like [abcd1234]) in your report.**
    38→The confirmation gate requires citing at least 10% of issues (or 5, whichever is smaller).
    39→
    40→**USE SUBAGENTS to parallelize this work.** Launch parallel subagents — one per dimension
    41→group — to investigate concurrently. Each subagent MUST:
    42→- Open and read the actual source file for EVERY assigned issue
    43→- Verify specific claims: count the actual casts, props, returns, line count
    44→- Check if the suggested fix already exists (common false positive)
    45→- Report a clear verdict per issue: genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering
    46→
    47→Example subagent split for 90 issues across 17 dimensions:
    48→- Subagent 1: architecture + organization (cross_module_architecture, package_organization, high_level_elegance)
    49→- Subagent 2: abstraction + design (abstraction_fitness, design_coherence, mid_level_elegance)
    50→- Subagent 3: duplicates + contracts (contract_coherence, api_surface_coherence, low_level_elegance)
    51→- Subagent 4: migrations + debt + conventions (incomplete_migration, ai_generated_debt, convention_outlier, naming_quality)
    52→- Subagent 5: type safety + errors + tests (type_safety, error_consistency, test_strategy, initialization_coupling, dependency_health)
    53→
    54→### Structured Assessment Template
    55→
    56→**Your report MUST include a structured assessment for EVERY issue.** Copy and fill out this
    57→template for each issue:
    58→
    59→```
    60→- hash: <issue hash>
    61→  verdict: genuine | false-positive | exaggerated | over-engineering
    62→  verdict_reasoning: <why this verdict — what you found when you read the code>
    63→  files_read: [<file paths the agent looked at>]
    64→  recommendation: <what to do about this issue>
    65→```
    66→
    67→**Example:**
    68→```
    69→- hash: 34580232
    70→  verdict: false-positive
    71→  verdict_reasoning: Uses branded string union KnownTaskType with ~25 literals in src/types/database.ts line 50. The issue describes code that doesn't exist.
    72→  files_read: [src/types/database.ts]
    73→  recommendation: No action needed — issue is inaccurate
    74→
    75→- hash: b634fc71
    76→  verdict: genuine
    77→  verdict_reasoning: Confirmed 65 properties at lines 217-282. Mixes pane lifecycle, filters, gallery data, interaction, and navigation.
    78→  files_read: [src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts]
    79→  recommendation: Decompose into focused sub-hooks
    80→```
    81→
    82→**Validation checks (all blocking):**
    83→- Every entry must have a recognized `verdict` keyword
    84→- Every entry must have non-empty `verdict_reasoning`
    85→- Every entry must have non-empty `files_read` list
    86→- Every entry must have non-empty `recommendation`
    87→
    88→**What a LAZY observe report looks like (will be rejected):**
    89→- "There are several convention issues that should be addressed"
    90→- "The type safety dimension has some genuine concerns"
    91→- Listing issue titles without any verification or independent analysis
    92→- Template fields left empty or with placeholder text
    93→
    94→{tail}
    95→"""
    96→
    97→
    98→def _reflect_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
    99→    tail = """\
   100→When done, run:
   101→```
   102→desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "<your strategy with cluster blueprint>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning dimensions or recurring patterns>"
   103→```
   104→"""
   105→    if mode == "output_only":
   106→        tail = """\
   107→When done, write a plain-text reflect report with a concrete cluster blueprint.
   108→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.
   109→"""
   110→    return f"""\
   111→## REFLECT Stage Instructions
   112→
   113→Your task: using the verdicts from observe, design the cluster structure.
   114→
   115→**A strategy is NOT a restatement of observe.** Observe says "here's what I found." Reflect
   116→says "here's what we should DO about it, and here's what we should NOT do, and here's WHY."
   117→
   118→**The Structured Observe Assessments table (provided below) is your primary input.** It contains
   119→a per-issue verdict (genuine/false-positive/exaggerated/over-engineering) with reasoning. Use
   120→these verdicts as authoritative — do not second-guess observe unless you have specific evidence.
   121→Issues with verdict `false-positive` or `over-engineering` should go into skip lines, not clusters.
   122→
   123→### What you must do:
   124→
   125→1. **Filter:** which issues are genuine (from the observe assessments table)?
   126→2. **Map:** for each genuine issue, what file/directory does it touch?
   127→3. **Group:** which issues share files or directories? These become clusters.
   128→4. **Skip:** which issues should be skipped? (with per-issue justification — "low priority" is
   129→   not a justification; "the fix would add a 50-line abstraction to save 3 lines of duplication" is)
   130→5. **Order:** which clusters depend on others? What's the execution sequence?
   131→6. **Check recurring patterns** — compare current issues against resolved history. If the same
   132→   dimension keeps producing issues, that's a root cause that needs addressing, not just
   133→   another round of fixes.
   134→7. **Account for every issue exactly once** — every open issue hash must appear in exactly one
   135→   cluster line or one skip line. Do not drop hashes, and do not repeat a hash in multiple
   136→   clusters or in both a cluster and a skip.
   137→
   138→### Your report MUST include both a coverage ledger and a concrete cluster blueprint
   139→
   140→This blueprint is what the organize stage will execute. Be specific:
   141→```
   142→## Coverage Ledger
   143→- a5996373 -> cluster "travel-structure-contract-unification"
   144→- fb113678 -> skip "false-positive-current-code"
   145→
   146→## Cluster Blueprint
   147→Cluster "media-lightbox-hooks" (all in src/domains/media-lightbox/)
   148→Cluster "task-typing" (both touch src/types/database.ts)
   149→
   150→## Skip Decisions
   151→Skip "false-positive-current-code" (false positive per observe)
   152→```
   153→
   154→### Hard accounting rule
   155→
   156→- Start your report with a `## Coverage Ledger` section.
   157→- In that section, mention each issue hash **once and only once** on its own ledger line.
   158→- Do **not** mention issue hashes again in cluster rationale paragraphs, recurring-pattern notes,
   159→  or ordering explanations. After the ledger, refer to clusters by name.
   160→- Before finishing, do a self-check: the ledger must cover all open issue hashes exactly once.
   161→
   162→### What a LAZY reflect looks like (will be rejected):
   163→- Restating observe findings in slightly different words
   164→- "We should prioritize high-impact items and defer low-priority ones"
   165→- A bulleted list of dimensions without any strategic thinking
   166→- Ignoring recurring patterns
   167→- No `## Coverage Ledger`
   168→- No cluster blueprint (just vague grouping ideas)
   169→- Missing or duplicated issue hashes
   170→
   171→### What a GOOD reflect looks like:
   172→- "50% false positive rate. Of 34 issues, 17 are genuine. 10 of those are batch-scriptable
   173→  convention fixes (zero risk, 30 min) — cluster 'convention-batch'. The remaining 7 split into
   174→  3 clusters by file proximity: 'media-lightbox-hooks' (issues X,Y,Z — all in src/domains/media-lightbox/),
   175→  'timeline-cleanup' (issues A,B,C — touching Timeline components), 'task-typing' (issues D,E).
   176→  Skip: issue W (false positive), issue V (over-engineering).
   177→  design_coherence recurs (2 resolved, 5 open) but only 1 of the 5 actually warrants work."
   178→
   179→{tail}
   180→"""
   181→
   182→
   183→def _organize_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   184→    intro = (
   185→        "The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go\n"
   186→        "where, what to skip. The reflect report included above is authoritative. Do not go\n"
   187→        "search old triage runs for a different blueprint unless you find a concrete mismatch.\n"
   188→        "Build it using the CLI. If something doesn't work as planned\n"
   189→        "(issue hash doesn't match, file proximity doesn't hold), adjust and document why."
   190→    )
   191→    process_block = """\
   192→2. **Skip issues that observe flagged as false-positive or over-engineering.** This is mandatory,
   193→   not optional. Check the **Structured Observe Assessments** table (provided below) — every
   194→   issue with verdict `false-positive` or `over-engineering` MUST be skipped. Use the observe
   195→   `verdict_reasoning` as the basis for your skip note:
   196→   ```
   197→   desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<reason from observe verdict>" --attest "I have reviewed this triage skip against the code and I am not gaming the score by suppressing a real defect."
   198→   ```
   199→   Do NOT cluster an issue that observe determined is not a real defect.
   200→3. Create clusters as specified in the blueprint:
   201→   `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
   202→4. Add issues: `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <patterns...>`
   203→5. Add steps that consolidate: one step per file or logical change, NOT one step per issue
   204→6. Set `--effort` on each step individually (trivial/small/medium/large)
   205→7. Set `--depends-on` when clusters touch overlapping files
   206→"""
   207→    tail = """\
   208→When done, run:
   209→```
   210→desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary of priorities and organization>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   211→```
   212→"""
   213→    if mode == "output_only":
   214→        intro = (
   215→            "The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go\n"
   216→            "where, what to skip. The reflect report included above is authoritative. Do not go\n"
   217→            "search old triage runs for a different blueprint unless you find a concrete mismatch.\n"
   218→            "Translate that plan into a precise organize report. If something\n"
   219→            "doesn't work as planned (issue hash doesn't match, file proximity doesn't hold), adjust\n"
   220→            "and document why."
   221→        )
   222→        process_block = """\
   223→2. **Skip issues that observe flagged as false-positive or over-engineering.** This is mandatory.
   224→   Check the **Structured Observe Assessments** table (provided below) — every issue with
   225→   verdict `false-positive` or `over-engineering` MUST be skipped. Use the observe
   226→   `verdict_reasoning` as the basis for your skip justification.
   227→   Do NOT cluster an issue that observe determined is not a real defect.
   228→3. Define the clusters exactly as they should be created.
   229→4. Assign every kept issue to a cluster.
   230→5. Consolidate steps: one step per file or logical change, NOT one step per issue.
   231→6. Assign an effort level to each planned step (trivial/small/medium/large).
   232→7. Call out cross-cluster dependencies when clusters touch overlapping files.
   233→"""
   234→        tail = """\
   235→When done, write a plain-text organize report that names the clusters, their issue membership,
   236→their consolidated steps, and any skip/dependency decisions. The orchestrator records the stage.
   237→"""
   238→    return f"""\
   239→## ORGANIZE Stage Instructions
   240→
   241→Your task: execute the cluster blueprint from the reflect stage.
   242→
   243→{intro}
   244→
   245→This stage should be largely mechanical. If you find yourself making major strategic
   246→decisions, something went wrong in reflect — the strategy should already be decided.
   247→
   248→### Process
   249→
   250→1. Review the reflect report's cluster blueprint AND the observe assessments table (both provided below)
   251→{process_block}
   252→
   253→### Quality gates (the confirmation will check these)
   254→
   255→Before recording, verify:
   256→- [ ] Every issue with observe verdict `false-positive` or `over-engineering` has been skipped
   257→- [ ] Every cluster name describes an area or specific change, not a problem type
   258→- [ ] No cluster has issues from 5+ unrelated directories (theme-group smell)
   259→- [ ] Step count < issue count (consolidation happened)
   260→- [ ] Every skip has a specific per-issue reason (not "low priority")
   261→- [ ] Overlapping clusters have --depends-on set
   262→- [ ] Cluster descriptions describe the WORK, not the PROBLEMS
   263→
   264→Every review issue must end up in a cluster OR be skipped.
   265→
   266→{tail}
   267→"""
   268→
   269→
   270→def _enrich_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   271→    subagent_block = """\
   272→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   273→
   274→1. Run `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` to get current steps and issue list
   275→2. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   276→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   277→3. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   278→   function name), and the exact change to make
   279→4. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   280→"""
   281→    tail = """\
   282→When done, run:
   283→```
   284→desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"

   289→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:

   291→1. Inspect the cluster definition provided in the prompt context

   303→## ENRICH Stage Instructions
   304→
   305→Your task: make EVERY step executor-ready. The test: could a developer who has never seen
   306→this codebase read your step detail and make the change without asking a single question?
   307→
   308→If the answer is "they'd need to figure out which file" or "they'd need to understand the
   309→context" — your step is not ready. Be specific enough that the work is mechanical.
   310→Use the organize report included above as the authoritative cluster plan; do not re-derive
   311→strategy from old triage runs unless you find a concrete mismatch you need to explain.
   312→
   313→### Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — confirmation will reject if not met)
   314→
   315→1. Every step MUST have `--detail` with 80+ chars INCLUDING at least one file path (src/... or supabase/...)
   316→2. Every step MUST have `--issue-refs` linking it to specific review issue hash(es)
   317→3. Every step MUST have `--effort` tag (trivial/small/medium/large) — set INDIVIDUALLY, not bulk
   318→4. File paths in detail MUST exist on disk (validator checks this)

   323→{subagent_block}
   324→
   325→### Common lazy patterns to avoid
   326→
   327→**Copying the issue description as step detail.** The issue says "useGenerationsPaneController
   328→returns 60+ values mixing concerns." That's a PROBLEM description. The step detail should say
   329→"In src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts (283 lines),
   330→extract lines 45-89 (filter state: activeFilter, setActiveFilter, filterOptions, applyFilter)
   331→into a new useGenerationFilters hook. The controller imports and spreads the sub-hook's return."
   332→

   334→"Extract lines 45-89 into useGenerationFilters", "delete lines 12-18", "rename the file
   335→from X to Y and update the 3 imports in A.tsx, B.tsx, C.tsx" are actions.
   336→
   337→**Guessing file paths.** If you write `src/shared/lib/jsonNarrowing.ts` and it doesn't exist,
   338→confirmation will block. READ the file system. Only reference files you've verified exist.
   339→
   340→**Bulk effort tags.** Don't mark everything "small". A file rename with 2 imports is "trivial".
   341→Decomposing a 400-line hook into 3 sub-hooks is "medium" or "large". Think about each one.
   342→

   348→to capture previous queryClient state before optimistic update. In onError callback, restore

   363→Do NOT use `--done-step` — steps are only marked done when actual code changes are made.
   364→
   365→### File path rules
   366→
   367→Only reference files that exist RIGHT NOW. Do not reference files that a step will create

   376→def _sense_check_instructions(mode: PromptMode = "self_record") -> str:
   377→    content_fix_block = (
   378→        'Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..." --effort <tag>`'
   379→    )
   380→    structure_fix_block = """\
   381→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other>`
   382→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --add-step "..." --detail "..." --effort trivial --issue-refs <hash>`
   383→"""
   384→    tail = """\
   385→When done, run:
   386→```

   388→```

   390→    if mode == "output_only":
   391→        content_fix_block = (
   392→            "Report the exact step corrections that need to be made; the orchestrator will apply them."
   393→        )

   395→            "Report the exact dependency additions or cascade steps that need to be made; "
   396→            "the orchestrator will apply them.\n"
   397→        )
   398→        tail = """\

   400→The orchestrator records and confirms the stage.

   402→    return f"""\
   403→## SENSE-CHECK Stage Instructions
   404→
   405→This stage is handled by two parallel subagents. If you are being run as a
   406→single-subprocess fallback, perform BOTH the content and structure checks below.
   407→
   408→### Content Check (per cluster)
   409→For EVERY step in every cluster, read the actual source file and verify:
   410→1. LINE NUMBERS: Does the code at the claimed lines match the step description?
   411→2. NAMES: Do function/variable/type names in the step exist in the file?
   412→3. COUNTS: Are counts ("update the 3 imports") accurate?
   413→4. STALENESS: Is the problem still present, or already fixed?
   414→5. VAGUENESS: Could a developer with zero context execute this step?
   415→6. EFFORT TAGS: Does the tag match actual scope?
   416→7. DUPLICATES: Flag steps that duplicate work in another cluster.
   417→8. OVER-ENGINEERING: Would this change make the codebase *worse*? Flag steps that:
   418→   - Add abstractions, wrappers, or indirection for a one-time operation
   419→   - Introduce unnecessary config/feature-flags/generalization
   420→   - Make simple code harder to read for marginal benefit
   421→   - Gold-plate beyond what the issue actually requires
   422→   - Trade one smell for a worse one (e.g. fix duplication by adding a fragile base class)
   423→   Remove or simplify over-engineered steps. If the whole cluster is net-negative, say so.
   424→
   425→{content_fix_block}
   426→
   427→### Structure Check (global)
   428→Build a file-touch graph and check:
   429→1. SHARED FILES: Two clusters touching same file without --depends-on → add dependency
   430→2. MISSING CASCADE: Rename/remove without importer updates → add cascade step
   431→3. CIRCULAR DEPS: Flag cycles, don't add them
   432→
   433→{structure_fix_block}
   434→
   435→{tail}
   436→"""

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    10→def build_sense_check_content_prompt(
    11→    *,
    12→    cluster_name: str,
    13→    plan: dict,
    14→    repo_root: Path,
    15→    policy_block: str = "",
    16→    mode: str = "output_only",

    43→
    44→    parts.append(
