```
   134→            "\n## Output\n"
   135→            "Write a plain-text summary of what you verified and what you changed in this cluster."
   136→        )
   137→    else:
   138→        parts.append(
   139→            "\n## Output\n"
   140→            "Write a plain-text report of your findings. The orchestrator records the stage."
   141→        )
   142→
   143→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   144→
   145→
   146→def build_sense_check_structure_prompt(
   147→    *,
   148→    plan: dict,
   149→    repo_root: Path,
   150→    mode: str = "output_only",
   151→    cli_command: str = "desloppify",

   153→    """Build a structure-verification prompt for cross-cluster dependency checking."""
   154→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})

   156→    parts: list[str] = []
   157→    parts.append(
   158→        "You are checking cross-cluster dependencies for the entire triage plan.\n"

   161→
   162→    parts.append(
   163→        "## Your job\n"
   164→        "Build a file-touch graph: for each cluster, which files do its steps reference?\n"
   165→        "Then check for unsafe relationships between clusters.\n"
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    parts.append(
   169→        "## What to check and fix\n"
   170→        "1. SHARED FILES: If cluster A and cluster B both have steps touching the same file,\n"
   171→        "   and neither depends on the other → report which dependency edge should be added.\n"
   172→        "2. MISSING CASCADE: If a step renames/removes a function or export, check whether\n"
   173→        "   any other file imports it. If those importers aren't covered by any step in any\n"
   174→        "   cluster → report the cascade step that should be added.\n"
   175→        "   Include the cluster name, affected importers, and issue hash in your report.\n"
   176→        "3. CIRCULAR DEPS: If adding a dependency would create a cycle, flag it in your report\n"
   177→        "   instead of adding it.\n"
   178→    )
   179→
   180→    if mode == "self_record":
   181→        parts.append(
   182→            "## How to apply structure fixes\n"
   183→            f"Use the exact CLI prefix: `{cli_command}`\n"
   184→            "Apply only structure-level mutations:\n"
   185→            f"- Add dependency edges: `{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other-cluster>`\n"
   186→            f"- Add missing cascade steps: `{cli_command} plan cluster update <name> --add-step \"...\" --detail \"...\" --effort <trivial|small|medium|large> --issue-refs <id...>`\n"
   187→        )
   188→        parts.append(
   189→            "## What NOT to do\n"

   191→            "- Do NOT change effort tags on existing steps\n"
   192→            "- Do NOT remove existing steps\n"
   193→            "- Do NOT run triage stage commands (`plan triage --stage ...`)\n"
   194→            "- Do NOT debug or repair the CLI / environment\n"
   195→        )
   196→    else:
   197→        parts.append(
   198→            "## What NOT to do\n"
   199→            "- Do NOT modify step detail text (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   200→            "- Do NOT change effort tags (the content subagent handles that)\n"
```

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me provide a comprehensive analysis:

## Analysis: Triage Helpers & Validation Complexity

### Code Volume Overview

**Total LOC across 9 core files: 3,225 lines**

Breaking down by function:
- **helpers.py** (main): 612 lines - metadata/state utilities
- **stages/helpers.py**: 117 lines - stage-gating functions  
- **validation/core.py**: 852 lines - complex ledger parsing + prerequisite logic
- **validation/enrich_checks.py**: 260 lines - step quality validators (11 distinct checks)
- **validation/enrich_quality.py**: 187 lines - quality report aggregation
- **validation/completion_stages.py**: 190 lines - completion flow gates
- **validation/completion_policy.py**: 190 lines - strategy/note validation
- **confirmations/basic.py**: 241 lines - attestation validation
- **stages/enrich.py**: 337 lines - enrich stage orchestration
- **stages/sense_check.py**: 246 lines - sense-check orchestration

### Key Finding: Validation Accounts for ~60% of Logic

The **validation/** and **confirmations/** directories contain approximately **1,920 lines**, which is **59% of the total**.

Core triage stage logic (helpers + stage orchestration): **~700 lines**

**This is disproportionate.**

### Validation Coverage Analysis

#### Enrich-level validators (11 distinct checks):

1. **`_underspecified_steps`** - Steps missing detail or issue_refs
2. **`_steps_with_bad_paths`** - File paths don't exist on disk
3. **`_steps_without_effort`** - Missing effort tags (trivial/small/medium/large)
4. **`_steps_missing_issue_refs`** - No issue_refs for traceability  
5. **`_steps_with_vague_detail`** - Detail too brief (<80 chars, no file paths)
6. **`_steps_referencing_skipped_issues`** - Points to wontfixed issues (stale refs)
7. **`_cluster_file_overlaps`** - Files referenced by multiple clusters
8. **`_clusters_with_directory_scatter`** - Issues span >5 unrelated directories
9. **`_clusters_with_high_step_ratio`** - Steps ≥ issues (1:1 mapping)
10. **`_enrich_report_or_error`** - Report length (100 char minimum)
11. **`_organize_report_or_error`** - Report length (100 char minimum)

#### Overlapping validations:

- **Enrich stage** runs `evaluate_enrich_quality()` with:
  - bad_paths_severity: "warning"
  - missing_effort_severity: "warning"
  - include_missing_issue_refs: False
  - include_vague_detail: False

- **Sense-check stage** runs `evaluate_enrich_quality()` with:
  - bad_paths_severity: "failure"
  - missing_effort_severity: "failure"
  - include_missing_issue_refs: True
  - include_vague_detail: True

This means the same validators run twice across different stages with different severity levels. They're **re-evaluated**, not **escalated**.

#### Completion validation:

- **`_completion_clusters_valid`** - Duplicates `unenriched_clusters()` checks
- **`_require_enrich_stage_for_complete`** - Re-checks `_underspecified_steps()`
- **`_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete`** - Re-checks `_underspecified_steps()` again
- **`_completion_strategy_valid`** - Validates strategy length (200 char minimum)
- **`_confirm_note_valid`** - Validates note length (100 char minimum)

The same `_underspecified_steps()` is called 3 separate times in the completion flow alone.

#### Attestation validation:

- **`validate_attestation()`** - 100 lines of dimension/cluster name reference checking
- Runs in: observe, reflect, organize, enrich, sense-check confirmations
- Each one validates that the attestation mentions at least one dimension or cluster name

#### Ledger validation in `core.py`:

- **`_walk_coverage_ledger()`** - 58 lines of regex parsing
- **`_resolve_token_to_id()`** - ID resolution with bucket fallback
- **`_analyze_reflect_issue_accounting()`** - Counts missing/duplicate issue citations
- **`_validate_reflect_issue_accounting()`** - Block if duplicates/missing
- **`validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger()`** - Check plan mutations match reflect dispositions

### Evaluation: Helpful vs. Bureaucratic

#### What's justified:

1. **Executor-readiness validation** - Steps need paths + specific instructions. This prevents wasted developer time. ✓
2. **Full coverage gates** - Every issue must be placed (clustered/skipped/dismissed). ✓
3. **Stage prerequisites** - Prevents out-of-order execution. ✓
4. **Attestation semantic checks** - Prevents users from copy-pasting boilerplate. ✓
5. **Reflect ledger matching** - Ensures organize faithfully materializes the plan. ✓

#### What's bureaucratic friction:

1. **Dual-severity validation** (enrich as warning, sense-check as failure) - Re-running the same `_underspecified_steps()`, `_steps_with_bad_paths()`, etc. across stages creates friction. The checks don't evolve between stages; they're just stricter.

2. **Report length gates** (100 chars minimum) - Force minimum verbosity without validating content quality. A user could write 101 chars of garbage.

3. **Unused validators running silently** - `_cluster_file_overlaps()`, `_clusters_with_directory_scatter()`, `_clusters_with_high_step_ratio()` are defined but **never called** (grep confirms they're only in the `__all__` export).

4. **Three separate underspecified checks in completion** - `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete()`, `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete()`, and the completion stage check all call `_underspecified_steps()` independently.

5. **Dimension/cluster name matching in attestations** - `_find_referenced_names()` does fuzzy case-insensitive matching ("my_dimension" ≈ "my dimension"). This is overly permissive—users could reference any word remotely close to a dimension name.

6. **Stale issue ref detection** (code checks `_steps_referencing_skipped_issues()`) - Runs at both enrich and sense-check stages, flagging issues that got marked wontfixed since organize. But this only triggers if `stale_issue_refs_severity` is set—it's silently off during enrich validation.

### Simpler Validation Approach

**Could reduce to ~50% of current lines without losing safety:**

1. **Collapse dual-severity checks** - Run each validator once at the terminal stage (sense-check). Enrich becomes "here's what we found" (informational), sense-check becomes "blocking failures."

2. **Remove unused validators** - Delete `_cluster_file_overlaps()`, `_clusters_with_directory_scatter()`, `_clusters_with_high_step_ratio()` (5 unused functions, ~65 lines).

3. **Deduplicate underspecified checks** - Call `_underspecified_steps()` once per completion attempt, not three times.

4. **Remove content-blind report length gates** - Instead validate that reports contain specific keywords (e.g., "I" + past-tense action words, or section headers like "## Clusters"). Or drop to 50 char minimum with spot-check sampling.

5. **Tighten attestation semantic checks** - Require exact cluster/dimension name matches (case-insensitive), not fuzzy substring matching. Validate that at least one cited item actually changed since the last triage.

6. **Consolidate ledger validation** - The 80-line ledger parser is sophisticated but only used in the reflect→organize gate. Consider: if dispositions exist, use them; otherwise, skip the check gracefully.

### Summary: Is the Complexity Justified?

**Partially.** About **65-70%** of the validation is legitimate guardrails preventing bad states. The remaining **30-35%** is redundant checking, silent no-op validators, and content-blind friction (report length gates).

The biggest culprit: **re-running the same structural checks at different severity levels** across stages instead of escalating them once at the end.

**Files involved:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/helpers.py` (612 LOC, foundational but dense)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/core.py` (852 LOC, ledger parsing dominates)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/enrich_checks.py` (260 LOC, 11 validators, 3 unused)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/completion_policy.py` (190 LOC, length gates)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/confirmations/basic.py` (241 LOC, attestation fuzzy matching)

Perfect! I now have a comprehensive understanding of the triage validation system. Let me provide a detailed analysis:

## Triage Validation System - Deep Understanding

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's a comprehensive understanding of the triage validation system:

### 1. **The Five Stages and Their Intent**

The triage system is a sequential 5-stage pipeline designed to move from **observation → strategy → execution → enrichment → final verification**.

#### **OBSERVE Stage**
- **Intent:** Verify every open review issue against actual source code
- **Problem it solves:** The review system has a high false-positive rate (~50%). Issues frequently miscount, describe already-fixed code, or suggest over-engineering
- **What it produces:** Structured assessments per issue with verdict (genuine/false-positive/exaggerated/over-engineering) + file citations
- **Validation gates:**
  - BLOCKING: Every issue must have a structured assessment with verdict, reasoning, files_read list, and recommendation
  - BLOCKING: Each assessment must cite specific files (not just restate issue titles)
  - BLOCKING: Must cite at least 10% of issues (or 5 minimum) by ID in the report
  - BLOCKING: Evidence parsing checks verdict keywords are recognized
  - ADVISORY: Encourages parallel subagents organized by dimension group
  - CRITICAL USER MESSAGE: "Don't confirm until analysis is backed by actual code evidence"

#### **REFLECT Stage**
- **Intent:** Design the cluster structure based on observe verdicts; decide what to fix vs. skip
- **Problem it solves:** Previous stages can't decide strategy; reflect makes that choice explicit
- **What it produces:** Coverage Ledger (each issue ID mentioned exactly once with decision: cluster or skip) + cluster blueprint
- **Key constraint:** Coverage Ledger must account for EVERY open issue exactly once - this is enforced by `_validate_reflect_issue_accounting()` which checks:
  - BLOCKING: No missing issue IDs
  - BLOCKING: No duplicate mentions
  - BLOCKING: Detects recurring patterns (issues in same dimension with both resolved and open cases = potential loop)
- **Validation gates:**
  - BLOCKING: Report must cite every open issue exactly once via the Coverage Ledger
  - BLOCKING: Must acknowledge recurring dimensions in the report
  - BLOCKING: Must provide concrete cluster blueprint, not vague grouping ideas
  - Advisory: Must distinguish between genuine issues (cluster) vs false positives/over-engineering (skip)

#### **ORGANIZE Stage**
- **Intent:** Execute the cluster blueprint from reflect by creating/populating clusters and skipping decided issues
- **Problem it solves:** Ensures reflect's decisions are actually materialized in plan state
- **What it produces:** Populated clusters with issues, skip list, organization report
- **Key validation mechanism: Disposition Ledger Matching** (`validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger()`)
  - Parses the reflect report's Coverage Ledger into structured `ReflectDisposition` objects
  - Compares against actual plan state (which clusters contain which issues, what's skipped)
  - BLOCKING: If any disposition doesn't match (e.g., reflect said cluster X but issue is in Y), blocks organize
  - Allows recovery without re-running reflect - just fix the divergence
- **Activity gate:** Requires minimum cluster operations (create/add/update/remove) since reflect to prove work happened (can be overridden with attestation)
- **Validation gates:**
  - BLOCKING: Manual clusters must exist (not just auto-clusters)
  - BLOCKING: All clusters must be enriched (have description + steps)
  - BLOCKING: Every review issue must be in a cluster OR skipped (no unplaced issues)
  - BLOCKING: Must respect reflect's disposition ledger
  - BLOCKING: Organize report must reference created clusters
  - ADVISORY: All false-positive/over-engineering issues from observe must be skipped

#### **ENRICH Stage**
- **Intent:** Make every step executor-ready with specific detail, effort tags, and issue refs
- **Problem it solves:** Steps are too vague for a developer to execute without asking questions
- **What it produces:** Fully detailed steps that include file paths, line ranges, specific actions
- **Quality checks** (`evaluate_enrich_quality()`):
  - BLOCKING: Every step needs `--detail` (80+ chars) including file path
  - BLOCKING: Every step needs `--issue-refs` (for traceability and auto-completion)
  - BLOCKING: Underspecified steps (missing detail OR missing issue_refs)
  - ADVISORY/WARNING: Bad file paths (don't exist on disk)
  - ADVISORY/WARNING: Missing effort tags (trivial/small/medium/large)
- **Activity gate:** Requires cluster_update operations since organize to prove enrichment happened
- **Validation gates:**
  - BLOCKING: Organize stage must be confirmed before enrich can run
  - BLOCKING: All above quality checks are blocking at confirmation
- **Goal:** "Could a developer with zero context execute this step without asking a question?"

#### **SENSE-CHECK Stage**
- **Intent:** Final verification that steps are accurate, executable, and not over-engineered
- **What it validates:**
  - **Content checks (per cluster):**
    - LINE NUMBERS: Code at claimed lines matches description
    - NAMES: Function/variable/type names exist
    - COUNTS: Accurate ("update 3 imports" - are there really 3?)
    - STALENESS: Problem still exists or already fixed?
    - VAGUENESS: Could executor do it without questions?
    - EFFORT TAGS: Realistic scope match
    - DUPLICATES: Same work in multiple clusters?
    - OVER-ENGINEERING: Would change make codebase worse?
  - **Structure checks (global):**
    - SHARED FILES: Two clusters touching same file without depends-on
    - MISSING CASCADE: Rename/remove without importer updates
    - CIRCULAR DEPS: Flag cycles
- **Quality report** (`evaluate_enrich_quality()` in "sense-check" mode):
  - BLOCKING: Underspecified steps
  - BLOCKING: Bad file paths
  - BLOCKING: Missing effort tags
  - BLOCKING: Missing issue_refs
  - BLOCKING: Vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths)
  - ADVISORY: Stale issue refs (point to skipped/wontfixed issues)
- **Validation gates:**
  - BLOCKING: Enrich stage must be confirmed before sense-check
  - BLOCKING: All quality checks must pass
  - BLOCKING: Evidence validation (must cite file paths if open review issues exist, must reference clusters)
- **Parallel structure:** Content and structure subagents run in parallel, each with specialized checks

---

### 2. **Coverage Ledger - The Accountability Mechanism**

The Coverage Ledger is the core innovation that ensures **every issue is accounted for exactly once**.

**Problem it solves:**
- Without it, issues can silently disappear (not clustered, not explicitly skipped)
- Prevents work slipping through without decision
- Makes strategy explicit and reviewable

**How it works:**
- Reflect stage MUST include a `## Coverage Ledger` section
- Each line lists an issue ID and its disposition: `- abcd1234 -> cluster "name"` or `- abcd1234 -> skip "reason"`
- Parser (`_walk_coverage_ledger()`) uses intelligent token resolution:
  - Accepts full IDs: `review::python::naming_quality::a5996373`
  - Accepts short hashes: `a5996373` (unique hex matching)
  - Accepts short tails: `a5996373` (if unique across valid IDs)
  - Falls back to scanning full ID in report
- **Disposition Ledger** is structured and persisted in plan.json for organize to validate against
- Parser is **forgiving on format** (supports variations: quoted targets, unquoted, bare arrows) but **strict on accounting** (every ID exactly once)

**Validation:**
- Reflect records `disposition_ledger` (list of ReflectDisposition objects)
- Organize validates plan state matches each disposition (right cluster, right skip status)
- `LedgerMismatch` objects report divergences clearly

---

### 3. **Strictness Levels: Failures vs Warnings**

The system distinguishes between **blocking failures** and **non-blocking warnings**:

#### **Blocking Failures (stop the stage):**
- Missing or duplicate issue IDs in Coverage Ledger (reflect)
- Unclustered or unplaced review issues (organize)
- Underspecified steps (enrich/sense-check) - missing detail/issue_refs
- Bad file paths that don't exist on disk (enrich confirmation stage)
- Vague step detail < 80 chars without file paths (sense-check)
- Unclustered review issues that aren't skipped (organize)
- Clusters with issues but missing description/steps (organize)
- Stage prerequisites not met (e.g., reflect before organize)
- Stage confirmations not attested (before downstream stages)

#### **Warnings (allow progression but alert):**
- Bad file paths (advisory at enrich, warning at confirmation, failure at sense-check)
- Missing effort tags (warning at enrich, failure at confirmation)
- Cluster file overlaps (organize enrich)
- High step-to-issue ratios (enrich)
- Clusters spanning 5+ unrelated directories (enrich)
- Stale issue refs pointing to skipped issues (warning in enrich, none in sense-check)
- Advisory evidence failures (recurring dimensions not mentioned, etc.)

#### **Auto-Confirmation Pattern:**
- Stages can use `--attestation` to auto-confirm prerequisites
- Organize can auto-confirm reflect if accounting validates + attestation provided
- Enrich can auto-confirm organize if good attestation provided
- Minimum attestation: 40 chars for activity overrides, MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN for stage confirmations

---

### 4. **Validation Philosophy**

The system embodies a **"verification over trust" philosophy**:

1. **Every claim must be backed by evidence:**
   - Observe: cite specific files
   - Reflect: account for every issue
   - Organize: materialize disposition decisions
   - Enrich: include file paths, effort, issue refs
   - Sense-check: verify facts against actual code

2. **Stage prerequisites enforce ordering:**
   - Can't organize without reflect
   - Can't enrich without confirmed organize
   - Can't sense-check without confirmed enrich
   - Can't complete without sense-check confirmed
   - This prevents "jumping ahead" with incomplete strategy

3. **Activity gates prove intent:**
   - Organize requires cluster operations since reflect
   - Enrich requires cluster_update operations since organize
   - Can override with attestation if justified

4. **Confirmation gates require explicit commitment:**
   - Each stage must be explicitly confirmed before downstream work
   - Confirmation requires a brief attestation text (MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN, typically 30-40 chars)
   - Clearing one stage's confirmation cascades to clear all downstream stages
   - Reusing a report in the same stage preserves prior confirmation automatically

---

### 5. **Key Data Structures**

- **`ReflectDisposition`:** Frozen dataclass representing one issue's intended decision (cluster/skip + target)
- **`ActualDisposition`:** Describes what actually happened to an issue in plan state (clustered/skipped/unplaced)
- **`LedgerMismatch`:** Reports divergence between intended and actual
- **`EnrichQualityIssue`:** Structured quality problem with severity (failure/warning)
- **`EnrichQualityReport`:** Collection of failures and warnings from enrich/sense-check evaluation

---

### 6. **High-Level Validation Flow**

```
OBSERVE → emit verdicts per issue
   ↓ (requires confirmation)
REFLECT → emit Coverage Ledger + cluster blueprint
   ↓ (validate accounting, parse dispositions)
ORGANIZE → materialize dispositions in plan state
   ↓ (validate reflect ledger matches plan state)
ENRICH → add detail/effort/refs to steps
   ↓ (quality checks: detail/effort/refs/paths all present)
SENSE-CHECK → verify accuracy + no over-engineering
   ↓ (quality checks: all above + vague detail + structure)
COMPLETE → freeze plan, clear triage stages, record strategy
```

Each arrow includes **validation gates** that block progression if conditions aren't met. The system is **linear but can rerun earlier stages** — doing so cascades clearing of confirmations in downstream stages, forcing re-confirmation.

This design ensures that **by the time completion happens, every issue has been verified, accounted for, organized into clusters, enriched with steps, and sense-checked for accuracy and execution feasibility.**

I need to design a plan to address three related GitHub issues (397, 398, 399) about the triage validation system being overzealous and creating friction. Here's the full context:

## The System
The triage pipeline has 5 stages: observe → reflect → organize → enrich → sense-check. Each stage has validation gates. The validation code is ~1,920 lines (59% of total triage code).

## The Three Issues

### Issue 399: Path validator incompatible with executor-ready steps
The `_steps_with_bad_paths()` function in `enrich_checks.py` extracts paths via regex `_PATH_RE = re.compile(r"(?:[\w./-]+/)?(?:src|supabase)/[\w./-]+\.\w+")` and checks if they exist. But executor-ready steps necessarily reference paths that don't exist yet (move destinations, files to create, line number suffixes like `file.ts:45`). The validator produces ~95% false positives. At sense-check stage, bad_paths_severity is "failure", blocking completion.

### Issue 398: Process friction for AI agents
Key friction points:
1. Coverage Ledger `->` format documented in reflect prompt but not clear enough
2. Issue ID citation in observe uses `if issue_id in report` requiring full IDs
3. Sense-check validates ALL clusters in plan, not just current triage cycle
4. Bad path errors show limited detail (5 clusters, 3 paths each)
5. Five mandatory stages heavy when clusters already exist
6. No `--report-file` option for long reports

### Issue 397: Executor-ready detail standard
Wants stricter content validation (paths, line numbers, specific identifiers in steps) but this directly conflicts with issue 399's path validator problem.

## Key Files
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/enrich_checks.py` (260 LOC) - path validation, step quality checks
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/enrich_quality.py` (~187 LOC) - quality report aggregation, calls enrich_checks
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/core.py` (852 LOC) - ledger parsing, issue ID validation
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/sense_check.py` (~246 LOC) - sense-check orchestration
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/enrich.py` (~337 LOC) - enrich orchestration
- CLI parser for triage: `desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_plan_impl_sections_triage_commit_scan.py`

## Current Severity Settings
- Enrich stage: bad_paths_severity="warning", missing_effort_severity="warning"
- Sense-check stage: bad_paths_severity="failure", missing_effort_severity="failure", include_missing_issue_refs=True, include_vague_detail=True

## Key Observations
1. Three unused validators exist: `_cluster_file_overlaps`, `_clusters_with_directory_scatter`, `_clusters_with_high_step_ratio`
2. `_underspecified_steps()` is called 3 separate times in the completion flow
3. The path regex doesn't handle line number suffixes, backtick-wrapped paths, glob patterns, or npm packages
4. Sense-check validates ALL clusters, not just ones from current triage

## My Assessment
The fundamental tension is: issue 397 wants MORE specificity in steps (exact paths, line numbers) while issue 399 shows the path validator can't handle that specificity. The solution is to make path validation smarter (strip line suffixes, skip creation destinations) and advisory rather than blocking, while keeping the content quality checks (detail length, issue_refs, effort tags) as blocking.

Please design a focused implementation plan that:
1. Fixes the path validator to handle real-world step descriptions (line suffixes, create/move destinations)
2. Makes path validation advisory (warning) at all stages, never blocking
3. Scopes sense-check to only validate current triage cycle clusters
4. Adds `--report-file` option
5. Removes unused validators
6. Deduplicates underspecified step checks in completion flow

Keep the plan focused and practical. Don't over-engineer.

```
    26→    """Return structured action steps for one cluster."""
    27→    return [
    28→        step
    29→        for step in (cluster.get("action_steps") or [])
    30→        if isinstance(step, dict)
    31→    ]
    32→
    33→
    34→def _detail_paths(detail: str) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Extract file paths from one step detail string."""
    36→    if not detail:
    37→        return []
    38→    return _PATH_RE.findall(detail)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _path_exists_or_alt_exists(repo_root: Path, path_str: str) -> bool:
    42→    """Return whether the referenced path or a ts/js sibling exists."""
    43→    path = repo_root / path_str
    44→    if path.exists():
    45→        return True
    46→    alt_ext = _EXT_SWAPS.get(path.suffix)
    47→    return bool(alt_ext and path.with_suffix(alt_ext).exists())
    48→
    49→
    50→def _require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages: dict) -> bool:
    51→    """Gate: organize must be done before enrich."""
    52→    from .core import require_stage_prerequisite  # noqa: PLC0415
    53→
    54→    return require_stage_prerequisite(
    55→        stages,
    56→        flow="enrich",
    57→        messages={
    58→            "observe": (
    59→                "  Cannot enrich: observe stage not complete.",
    60→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."',
    61→            ),
    62→            "reflect": (
    63→                "  Cannot enrich: reflect stage not complete.",
    64→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."',
    65→            ),
    66→            "organize": (
    67→                "  Cannot enrich: organize stage not complete.",
    68→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "..."',
    69→            ),
    70→        },
    71→    )
    72→
    73→
    74→def _underspecified_steps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
    75→    """Return (cluster_name, bare_count, total_count) for steps missing detail or issue_refs."""
    76→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
    77→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
    78→        steps = _cluster_steps(cluster)
    79→        if not steps:
    80→            continue
    81→        bare = sum(
    82→            1
    83→            for step in steps
    84→            if isinstance(step, dict) and (not step.get("detail") or not step.get("issue_refs"))
    85→        )
    86→        if bare > 0:
    87→            results.append((name, bare, len(steps)))
    88→    return results
    89→
    90→
    91→def _steps_with_bad_paths(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
    92→    """Return steps referencing file paths that don't exist on disk."""
    93→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
    94→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
    95→        for i, step in enumerate(_cluster_steps(cluster)):
    96→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
    97→            if not detail:
    98→                continue
    99→            bad = [
   100→                path_str
   101→                for path_str in _detail_paths(detail)
   102→                if not _path_exists_or_alt_exists(repo_root, path_str)
   103→            ]
   104→            if bad:
   105→                results.append((name, i + 1, bad))
   106→    return results
   107→
   108→
   109→def _steps_without_effort(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   110→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without effort tags."""
   111→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   112→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   113→        steps = _cluster_steps(cluster)
   114→        if not steps:
   115→            continue
   116→        missing = sum(
   117→            1 for step in steps if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("effort") not in _VALID_EFFORTS
   118→        )
   119→        if missing:
   120→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   121→    return results
   122→
   123→
   124→def _cluster_file_overlaps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]]:
   125→    """Return pairs of clusters with overlapping file references in step details."""
   126→    cluster_files: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   127→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   128→        paths: set[str] = set()
   129→        for step in _cluster_steps(cluster):
   130→            paths.update(_detail_paths(step.get("detail", "")))
   131→        if paths:
   132→            cluster_files[name] = paths
   133→
   134→    overlaps: list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]] = []
   135→    names = sorted(cluster_files.keys())
   136→    for i, left in enumerate(names):
   137→        for right in names[i + 1 :]:
   138→            shared = cluster_files[left] & cluster_files[right]
   139→            if shared:
   140→                overlaps.append((left, right, sorted(shared)))
   141→    return overlaps
   142→
   143→
   144→def _clusters_with_directory_scatter(
   145→    plan: dict,
   146→    *,
   147→    threshold: int = 5,
   148→) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   149→    """Return clusters whose issues span too many unrelated directories."""
   150→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   151→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   152→        dirs: set[str] = set()
   153→        for step in _cluster_steps(cluster):
   154→            for path_str in _detail_paths(step.get("detail", "")):
   155→                parts = path_str.split("/")
   156→                if len(parts) >= 3:
   157→                    dirs.add("/".join(parts[:3]))
   158→                elif len(parts) >= 2:
   159→                    dirs.add("/".join(parts[:2]))
   160→        if len(dirs) >= threshold:
   161→            results.append((name, len(dirs), sorted(dirs)[:6]))
   162→    return results
   163→
   164→
   165→def _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(
   166→    plan: dict,
   167→    *,
   168→    max_ratio: float = 1.0,
   169→) -> list[tuple[str, int, int, float]]:
   170→    """Return clusters where step count >= issue count (1:1 mapping)."""
   171→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int, float]] = []
   172→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   173→        steps = len(_cluster_steps(cluster))
   174→        issues = len(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
   175→        if issues >= 3 and steps > 0:
   176→            ratio = steps / issues
   177→            if ratio > max_ratio:
   178→                results.append((name, steps, issues, ratio))
   179→    return results
   180→
   181→
   182→def _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   183→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without issue_refs."""
   184→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   185→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   186→        steps = _cluster_steps(cluster)
   187→        if not steps:
   188→            continue
   189→        missing = sum(1 for step in steps if not step.get("issue_refs"))
   190→        if missing:
   191→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   192→    return results
   193→
   194→
   195→def _steps_with_vague_detail(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
   196→    """Return steps with detail too vague to be executor-ready."""
   197→    del repo_root
   198→    results: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
   199→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   200→        for i, step in enumerate(_cluster_steps(cluster)):
   201→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   202→            if not detail:
   203→                results.append((name, i + 1, step.get("title", "(no title)")))
   204→                continue
   205→            has_path = bool(_detail_paths(detail))
   206→            if len(detail) < 80 and not has_path:
   207→                results.append((name, i + 1, step.get("title", "(no title)")))
   208→    return results
   209→
   210→
   211→def _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   212→    """Return steps whose issue_refs include wontfixed/skipped issues."""
   213→    wontfixed = set()
   214→    for fid, issue in plan.get("issues", {}).items():
   215→        if isinstance(issue, dict) and issue.get("status") in ("wontfix", "skipped"):
   216→            wontfixed.add(fid)
   217→    for fid in plan.get("wontfix", {}):
   218→        wontfixed.add(fid)
   219→
   220→    if not wontfixed:
   221→        return []
   222→
   223→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   224→    for name, cluster in _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan):
   225→        for i, step in enumerate(_cluster_steps(cluster)):
   226→            refs = step.get("issue_refs") or []
   227→            stale = [ref for ref in refs if ref in wontfixed]
   228→            if stale:
   229→                results.append((name, i + 1, stale))
   230→    return results
   231→
   232→
   233→def _enrich_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   234→    if not report:
   235→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage enrich.", "red"))
   236→        print(colorize("  Summarize the enrichment work you did:", "dim"))
   237→        print(colorize("  - Which clusters did you add detail/refs to?", "dim"))
   238→        print(colorize("  - Are steps specific enough for an executor with zero context?", "dim"))
   239→        print(colorize("  - Did you link issue_refs so steps auto-complete on resolve?", "dim"))
   240→        return None
   241→    if len(report) < 100:
   242→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   243→        print(colorize("  Explain what enrichment you did and why steps are executor-ready.", "dim"))
   244→        return None
   245→    return report
   246→
   247→
   248→__all__ = [
   249→    "_cluster_file_overlaps",
   250→    "_clusters_with_directory_scatter",
   251→    "_clusters_with_high_step_ratio",
   252→    "_enrich_report_or_error",
   253→    "_require_organize_stage_for_enrich",
   254→    "_steps_missing_issue_refs",
   255→    "_steps_referencing_skipped_issues",
   256→    "_steps_with_bad_paths",
   257→    "_steps_with_vague_detail",
   258→    "_steps_without_effort",
   259→    "_underspecified_steps",
   260→]
   261→
```

```
1→"""Shared enrich/sense-check quality evaluation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Literal
     8→
     9→from .enrich_checks import (
    10→    _steps_missing_issue_refs,
    11→    _steps_referencing_skipped_issues,
    12→    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    13→    _steps_with_vague_detail,
    14→    _steps_without_effort,
    15→    _underspecified_steps,
    16→)
    17→
    18→Severity = Literal["failure", "warning"]
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class EnrichQualityIssue:
    23→    """Structured enrich-quality issue with severity and source rows."""
    24→
    25→    code: str
    26→    total: int
    27→    rows: list[tuple]
    28→    message: str
    29→    severity: Severity
    30→
    31→
    32→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    33→class EnrichQualityReport:
    34→    """Structured enrich-quality result for stage flows, confirmations, and runners."""
    35→
    36→    failures: list[EnrichQualityIssue]
    37→    warnings: list[EnrichQualityIssue]
    38→
    39→    def failure(self, code: str) -> EnrichQualityIssue | None:
    40→        for issue in self.failures:
    41→            if issue.code == code:
    42→                return issue
    43→        return None
    44→
    45→    def warning(self, code: str) -> EnrichQualityIssue | None:
    46→        for issue in self.warnings:
    47→            if issue.code == code:
    48→                return issue
    49→        return None
    50→
    51→
    52→def _append_issue(
    53→    issues: list[EnrichQualityIssue],
    54→    *,
    55→    code: str,
    56→    total: int,
    57→    rows: list[tuple],
    58→    message: str,
    59→    severity: Severity,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    if total <= 0:
    62→        return
    63→    issues.append(
    64→        EnrichQualityIssue(
    65→            code=code,
    66→            total=total,
    67→            rows=rows,
    68→            message=message,
    69→            severity=severity,
    70→        )
    71→    )
    72→
    73→
    74→def evaluate_enrich_quality(
    75→    plan: dict,
    76→    repo_root: Path,
    77→    *,
    78→    phase_label: str,
    79→    bad_paths_severity: Severity,
    80→    missing_effort_severity: Severity,
    81→    include_missing_issue_refs: bool,
    82→    include_vague_detail: bool,
    83→    stale_issue_refs_severity: Severity | None,
    84→) -> EnrichQualityReport:
    85→    """Evaluate executor-readiness checks for enrich-level stage data."""
    86→    failures: list[EnrichQualityIssue] = []
    87→    warnings: list[EnrichQualityIssue] = []
    88→    sink = {"failure": failures, "warning": warnings}
    89→    sense_suffix = f" after {phase_label}" if phase_label == "sense-check" else ""
    90→
    91→    underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
    92→    _append_issue(
    93→        sink["failure"],
    94→        code="underspecified",
    95→        total=sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec),
    96→        rows=underspec,
    97→        message=f"{sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs{sense_suffix}.",
    98→        severity="failure",
    99→    )
   100→
   101→    bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
   102→    total_bad_paths = sum(len(paths) for _, _, paths in bad_paths)
   103→    bad_paths_message = (
   104→        f"{total_bad_paths} file path(s) don't exist on disk{sense_suffix}."
   105→        if phase_label == "sense-check"
   106→        else f"{total_bad_paths} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk."
   107→    )
   108→    _append_issue(
   109→        sink[bad_paths_severity],
   110→        code="bad_paths",
   111→        total=total_bad_paths,
   112→        rows=bad_paths,
   113→        message=bad_paths_message,
   114→        severity=bad_paths_severity,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    missing_effort = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   118→    total_missing_effort = sum(n for _, n, _ in missing_effort)
   119→    missing_effort_message = (
   120→        f"{total_missing_effort} step(s) have no effort tag{sense_suffix}."
   121→        if phase_label == "sense-check"
   122→        else f"{total_missing_effort} step(s) have no effort tag (trivial/small/medium/large)."
   123→    )
   124→    _append_issue(
   125→        sink[missing_effort_severity],
   126→        code="missing_effort",
   127→        total=total_missing_effort,
   128→        rows=missing_effort,
   129→        message=missing_effort_message,
   130→        severity=missing_effort_severity,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    if include_missing_issue_refs:
   134→        missing_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   135→        total_missing_refs = sum(n for _, n, _ in missing_refs)
   136→        missing_refs_message = (
   137→            f"{total_missing_refs} step(s) have no issue_refs{sense_suffix}."
   138→            if phase_label == "sense-check"
   139→            else f"{total_missing_refs} step(s) have no issue_refs for traceability."
   140→        )
   141→        _append_issue(
   142→            sink["failure"],
   143→            code="missing_issue_refs",
   144→            total=total_missing_refs,
   145→            rows=missing_refs,
   146→            message=missing_refs_message,
   147→            severity="failure",
   148→        )
   149→
   150→    if include_vague_detail:
   151→        vague_detail = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
   152→        vague_message = (
   153→            f"{len(vague_detail)} step(s) have vague detail{sense_suffix}."
   154→            if phase_label == "sense-check"
   155→            else (
   156→                f"{len(vague_detail)} step(s) have vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths). "
   157→                "Executor-ready means: file path + specific instruction."
   158→            )
   159→        )
   160→        _append_issue(
   161→            sink["failure"],
   162→            code="vague_detail",
   163→            total=len(vague_detail),
   164→            rows=vague_detail,
   165→            message=vague_message,
   166→            severity="failure",
   167→        )
   168→
   169→    if stale_issue_refs_severity is not None:
   170→        stale_refs = _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan)
   171→        total_stale_refs = sum(len(refs) for _, _, refs in stale_refs)
   172→        _append_issue(
   173→            sink[stale_issue_refs_severity],
   174→            code="stale_issue_refs",
   175→            total=total_stale_refs,
   176→            rows=stale_refs,
   177→            message=(
   178→                f"{total_stale_refs} step issue_ref(s) point to skipped/wontfixed issues"
   179→                f"{sense_suffix}. Remove stale refs."
   180→            ),
   181→            severity=stale_issue_refs_severity,
   182→        )
   183→
   184→    return EnrichQualityReport(failures=failures, warnings=warnings)
   185→
   186→
   187→__all__ = ["EnrichQualityIssue", "EnrichQualityReport", "evaluate_enrich_quality"]
   188→
```

```
    62→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    63→class AutoConfirmStageRequest:
    64→    """Configuration for one fold-confirm stage auto-confirmation."""
    65→

    74→
    75→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    76→class StagePrerequisite:
    77→    """One required upstream stage for a triage workflow seam."""
    78→
    79→    stage_name: str
    80→    require_confirmation: bool = False
    81→
    82→
    83→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    84→class ReflectAutoConfirmDeps:

    88→    detect_recurring_patterns_fn: object = detect_recurring_patterns
    89→    save_plan_fn: object | None = None
    90→
    91→
    92→_STAGE_PREREQUISITES = {
    93→    "organize": (
    94→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    95→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
    96→    ),
    97→    "enrich": (
    98→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),
    99→        StagePrerequisite("reflect"),
   100→        StagePrerequisite("organize"),
   101→    ),
   102→    "sense-check": (
   103→        StagePrerequisite("enrich", require_confirmation=True),
   104→    ),
   105→    "complete:organize": (
   106→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),

   108→    ),
   109→    "complete:enrich": (
   110→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),

   112→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
   113→    ),
   114→    "complete:sense-check": (
   115→        StagePrerequisite("observe"),

   117→        StagePrerequisite("enrich"),
   118→        StagePrerequisite("sense-check"),
   119→    ),
   120→}
   121→
   122→
   123→def _missing_stage_prerequisite(
   124→    stages: dict,
   125→    *,
   126→    flow: str,
   127→) -> StagePrerequisite | None:
   128→    """Return the first missing upstream stage required by one triage flow."""
   129→    for prerequisite in _STAGE_PREREQUISITES.get(flow, ()):
   130→        stage_record = stages.get(prerequisite.stage_name)
   131→        if stage_record is None:
   132→            return prerequisite
   133→        if prerequisite.require_confirmation and not stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
   134→            return prerequisite
   135→    return None
   136→
   137→
   138→def require_stage_prerequisite(
   139→    stages: dict,
   140→    *,
   141→    flow: str,
   142→    messages: dict[str, tuple[str, str]],
   143→) -> bool:
   144→    """Print consistent prerequisite guidance for simple triage stage gates."""
   145→    missing = _missing_stage_prerequisite(stages, flow=flow)
   146→    if missing is None:
   147→        return True
   148→    blocked_heading, command_hint = messages[missing.stage_name]
   149→    print(colorize(blocked_heading, "red"))
   150→    print(colorize(command_hint, "dim"))
   151→    return False
   152→
   153→
   154→def _auto_confirm_stage(
   155→    *,
   156→    plan: dict,
   157→    stage_record: dict,
   158→    attestation: str | None,
   159→    request: AutoConfirmStageRequest,
   160→    save_plan_fn=None,
   161→) -> bool:
   162→    """Shared auto-confirm flow for stage fold-confirm operations."""
   163→    if save_plan_fn is None:
   164→        save_plan_fn = save_plan
   165→    if stage_record.get("confirmed_at"):
   166→        return True
   167→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   168→        print(colorize(f"  {request.blocked_heading}", "red"))
   169→        print(colorize(f"  Run: {request.confirm_cmd}", "dim"))
   170→        print(colorize(f"  {request.inline_hint}", "dim"))
   171→        return False
   172→
   173→    confirmed_text = attestation.strip()
   174→    validation_err = validate_attestation(
   175→        confirmed_text,
   176→        request.stage_name,
   177→        dimensions=request.dimensions,
   178→        cluster_names=request.cluster_names,
   179→    )
   180→    if validation_err:
   181→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   182→        return False
   183→
   184→    stage_record["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   185→    stage_record["confirmed_text"] = confirmed_text
   186→    save_plan_fn(plan)
   187→    print(colorize(f"  ✓ {request.stage_label} auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   188→    return True
   189→
   190→
   191→def _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   192→    *,
   193→    plan: dict,
   194→    stages: dict,
   195→    attestation: str | None,
   196→    triage_input,
   197→    save_plan_fn=None,
   198→) -> bool:
   199→    observe_stage = stages.get("observe")
   200→    if observe_stage is None:
   201→        return False
   202→    _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   203→    return _auto_confirm_stage(
   204→        plan=plan,
   205→        stage_record=observe_stage,
   206→        attestation=attestation,
   207→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   208→            stage_name="observe",
   209→            stage_label="Observe",
   210→            blocked_heading="Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.",
   211→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm observe",
   212→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.",
   213→            dimensions=dim_names,
   214→        ),
   215→        save_plan_fn=save_plan_fn,
   216→    )
   217→
   218→
   219→def _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
   220→    *,
   221→    report: str,
   222→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   223→    recurring: dict,
   224→) -> bool:
   225→    if not recurring_dims:
   226→        return True
   227→    report_lower = report.lower()
   228→    mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
   229→    if mentioned:
   230→        return True
   231→    print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
   232→    for dim in recurring_dims:
   233→        info = recurring[dim]
   234→        print(
   235→            colorize(
   236→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
   237→                f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
   238→                "yellow",
   239→            )
   240→        )
   241→    print(colorize("  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.", "dim"))
   242→    return False
   243→
   244→
   245→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246→# Disposition types
   247→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248→
   249→DecisionKind = Literal["cluster", "permanent_skip"]
   250→
   251→
   252→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   253→class ReflectDisposition:
   254→    """One issue's intended disposition as declared by the reflect stage."""
   255→
   256→    issue_id: str
   257→    decision: DecisionKind
   258→    target: str  # cluster name or skip reason tag
   259→
   260→    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
   261→        """Serialize for JSON persistence in plan.json."""
   262→        return {"issue_id": self.issue_id, "decision": self.decision, "target": self.target}
   263→
   264→    @classmethod
   265→    def from_dict(cls, d: dict | ReflectDisposition) -> ReflectDisposition:
   266→        """Deserialize from persisted plan.json dict, or pass through if already typed."""
   267→        if isinstance(d, cls):
   268→            return d
   269→        return cls(
   270→            issue_id=d.get("issue_id", ""),
   271→            decision=d.get("decision", "cluster"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   272→            target=d.get("target", ""),
   273→        )
   274→
   275→
   276→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   277→class ActualDisposition:
   278→    """What actually happened to an issue in plan state."""
   279→
   280→    kind: Literal["clustered", "skipped", "unplaced"]
   281→    cluster_name: str = ""  # non-empty when kind == "clustered"
   282→
   283→    def describe(self, intended: ReflectDisposition | None = None) -> str:
   284→        """Human-readable description for error messages."""
   285→        if self.kind == "skipped":
   286→            return "permanently skipped"
   287→        if self.kind == "clustered":
   288→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster" and self.cluster_name != intended.target:
   289→                return f'in cluster "{self.cluster_name}" (expected "{intended.target}")'
   290→            return f'clustered in "{self.cluster_name}"'
   291→        if self.kind == "unplaced":
   292→            if intended and intended.decision == "cluster":
   293→                return "not in any cluster"
   294→            return "not skipped, not clustered"
   295→        return "unknown state"
   296→
   297→
   298→def _build_actual_disposition_index(plan: dict) -> dict[str, ActualDisposition]:
   299→    """Build a lookup from issue ID to its actual disposition in plan state."""
   300→    index: dict[str, ActualDisposition] = {}
   301→
   302→    for cluster_name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   303→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   304→            continue
   305→        for fid in cluster_issue_ids(cluster):
   306→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="clustered", cluster_name=cluster_name)
   307→
   308→    for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}):
   309→        if isinstance(fid, str):
   310→            index[fid] = ActualDisposition(kind="skipped")
   311→
   312→    return index
   313→
   314→
   315→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   316→# Coverage Ledger parsing — shared infrastructure
   317→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   318→
   319→def _build_id_resolution_maps(
   320→    valid_ids: set[str],
   321→) -> tuple[dict[str, list[str]], dict[str, str]]:
   322→    """Build short-ID lookup structures from a set of full issue IDs."""
   323→    short_id_buckets: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   324→    short_hex_map: dict[str, str] = {}
   325→    for issue_id in sorted(valid_ids):
   326→        short_id = issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   327→        short_id_buckets.setdefault(short_id, []).append(issue_id)
   328→        if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", short_id):
   329→            existing = short_hex_map.get(short_id)
   330→            if existing is None:
   331→                short_hex_map[short_id] = issue_id
   332→            elif existing != issue_id:
   333→                short_hex_map.pop(short_id, None)
   334→    return short_id_buckets, short_hex_map
   335→
   336→
   337→def _clean_ledger_token(raw: str) -> str:
   338→    """Strip backticks, brackets, and whitespace from a ledger token."""
   339→    token = raw.strip().strip("`").strip()
   340→    if token.startswith("[") and token.endswith("]"):
   341→        token = token[1:-1].strip()
   342→    return token
   343→
   344→
   345→def _extract_ledger_entry(line: str) -> tuple[str, str | None, str | None]:
   346→    """Parse a ledger line into (token, decision, target).
   347→
   348→    Always extracts the token (issue key) when a ``->`` is present.
   349→    Decision and target are None when the right-hand side doesn't match
   350→    the ``decision "target"`` pattern (e.g. ``-> TODO``).
   351→    Returns ``("", None, None)`` when the line has no ``->``.
   352→    """
   353→    # Try full structured form: - token -> decision "target"
   354→    match = re.match(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(\w+)\s+[\"']([^\"']+)[\"']", line)
   355→    if match:
   356→        [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   357→        return token, match.group(2).strip().lower(), match.group(3).strip()
   358→
   359→    # Try unquoted: - token -> decision target-slug
   360→    match = re.match(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(\w+)\s+(\S+.*)", line)
   361→    if match:
   362→        [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   363→        target = match.group(3).strip().strip("\"'")
   364→        return token, match.group(2).strip().lower(), target
   365→
   366→    # Bare arrow: - token -> (anything or nothing)
   367→    match = re.match(r"-\s*(.+?)\s*->", line)
   368→    if match:
   369→        [REDACTED](match.group(1))
   370→        return token, None, None
   371→
   372→    return "", None, None
   373→
   374→
   375→def _resolve_token_to_id(
   376→    token: str,
   377→    valid_ids: set[str],
   378→    short_id_buckets: dict[str, list[str]],
   379→    short_hex_map: dict[str, str],
   380→    short_id_usage: Counter[str],
   381→) -> str | None:
   382→    """Resolve a ledger token to a full issue ID, or None."""
   383→    if token in valid_ids:
   384→        return token
   385→    bucket = short_id_buckets.get(token)
   386→    if bucket:
   387→        bucket_index = short_id_usage[token]
   388→        issue_id = bucket[bucket_index] if bucket_index < len(bucket) else bucket[-1]
   389→        short_id_usage[token] += 1
   390→        return issue_id
   391→    # Hex-substring fallback
   392→    for hex_token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", token):
   393→        resolved = short_hex_map.get(hex_token)
   394→        if resolved:
   395→            return resolved
   396→    return None
   397→
   398→
   399→# Canonical decision values for dispositions
   400→_CLUSTER_DECISIONS = frozenset({"cluster"})
   401→_SKIP_DECISIONS = frozenset({"skip", "dismiss", "defer", "drop", "remove"})
   402→
   403→
   404→def _normalize_decision(raw: str) -> str:
   405→    """Normalize a ledger decision to 'cluster' or 'permanent_skip'."""
   406→    lower = raw.lower()
   407→    if lower in _CLUSTER_DECISIONS:
   408→        return "cluster"
   409→    if lower in _SKIP_DECISIONS:
   410→        return "permanent_skip"
   411→    return lower
   412→
   413→
   414→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   415→# Single-pass ledger walker — produces both accounting and dispositions
   416→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   417→
   418→@dataclass
   419→class _LedgerParseResult:
   420→    """Combined output of a single pass over the Coverage Ledger section."""
   421→
   422→    hits: Counter[str]  # issue_id -> mention count (for accounting)
   423→    dispositions: list[ReflectDisposition]  # structured dispositions
   424→    found_section: bool  # True if ## Coverage Ledger was present
   425→
   426→
   427→def _walk_coverage_ledger(
   428→    report: str,
   429→    valid_ids: set[str],
   430→) -> _LedgerParseResult:
   431→    """Single-pass parse of the Coverage Ledger section.
   432→
   433→    Extracts both hit counts (for accounting validation) and structured
   434→    dispositions (for the execution contract) in one walk.
   435→    """
   436→    short_id_buckets, short_hex_map = _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids)
   437→    hits: Counter[str] = Counter()
   438→    dispositions: list[dict] = []
   439→    short_id_usage: Counter[str] = Counter()
   440→    in_ledger = False
   441→    found_section = False
   442→
   443→    for raw_line in report.splitlines():
   444→        line = raw_line.strip()
   445→        if re.fullmatch(r"##\s+Coverage Ledger", line, re.IGNORECASE):
   446→            in_ledger = True
   447→            found_section = True
   448→            continue
   449→        if in_ledger and re.match(r"##\s+", line):
   450→            break
   451→        if not in_ledger:
   452→            continue
   453→
   454→        token, decision, target = _extract_ledger_entry(line)
   455→        if not token:
   456→            continue
   457→
   458→        issue_id = _resolve_token_to_id(
   459→            token, valid_ids, short_id_buckets, short_hex_map, short_id_usage,
   460→        )
   461→        if not issue_id:
   462→            # Last resort: scan entire line for hex IDs
   463→            for hex_token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", line):
   464→                resolved = short_hex_map.get(hex_token)
   465→                if resolved:
   466→                    issue_id = resolved
   467→                    break
   468→
   469→        if issue_id:
   470→            hits[issue_id] += 1
   471→            if decision and target:
   472→                normalized = _normalize_decision(decision)
   473→                if normalized in ("cluster", "permanent_skip"):
   474→                    dispositions.append(ReflectDisposition(
   475→                        issue_id=issue_id,
   476→                        decision=normalized,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   477→                        target=target,
   478→                    ))
   479→
   480→    return _LedgerParseResult(
   481→        hits=hits,
   482→        dispositions=dispositions,
   483→        found_section=found_section,
   484→    )
   485→
   486→
   487→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   488→# Public API — thin wrappers over the shared walker
   489→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   490→
   491→def parse_reflect_dispositions(
   492→    report: str,
   493→    valid_ids: set[str],
   494→) -> list[ReflectDisposition]:
   495→    """Parse the Coverage Ledger into structured dispositions.
   496→
   497→    Returns an empty list if no Coverage Ledger section is found.
   498→    """
   499→    return _walk_coverage_ledger(report, valid_ids).dispositions
   500→
   501→
   502→def _analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   503→    *,
   504→    report: str,
   505→    valid_ids: set[str],
   506→) -> tuple[set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   507→    """Return cited, missing, and duplicate issue IDs referenced by reflect."""
   508→    result = _walk_coverage_ledger(report, valid_ids)
   509→
   510→    if result.found_section and result.hits:
   511→        cited = set(result.hits)
   512→        duplicates = sorted(
   513→            issue_id for issue_id, count in result.hits.items() if count > 1
   514→        )
   515→        missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   516→        return cited, missing, duplicates
   517→
   518→    # Fallback for reports without a Coverage Ledger section
   519→    short_id_buckets, short_hex_map = _build_id_resolution_maps(valid_ids)
   520→    cited = extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
   521→    for issue_id in valid_ids:
   522→        if issue_id in report:
   523→            cited.add(issue_id)
   524→    short_hits = Counter(
   525→        short_hex_map[token]
   526→        for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", report)
   527→        if token in short_hex_map
   528→    )
   529→    duplicates = sorted(
   530→        issue_id for issue_id, count in short_hits.items() if count > 1
   531→    )
   532→    missing = sorted(valid_ids - cited)
   533→    return cited, missing, duplicates
   534→
   535→
   536→def _validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   537→    *,
   538→    report: str,
   539→    valid_ids: set[str],
   540→) -> tuple[bool, set[str], list[str], list[str]]:
   541→    """Ensure the reflect blueprint accounts for every open review issue exactly once."""
   542→    cited, missing, duplicates = _analyze_reflect_issue_accounting(
   543→        report=report,
   544→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   545→    )
   546→    if not missing and not duplicates:
   547→        return True, cited, missing, duplicates
   548→    print(
   549→        colorize(
   550→            "  Reflect report must account for every open review issue exactly once.",
   551→            "red",
   552→        )
   553→    )
   554→    if missing:
   555→        missing_short = ", ".join(issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in missing[:10])
   556→        print(colorize(f"    Missing: {missing_short}", "yellow"))
   557→    if duplicates:
   558→        duplicate_short = ", ".join(
   559→            issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1] for issue_id in duplicates[:10]
   560→        )
   561→        print(colorize(f"    Duplicated: {duplicate_short}", "yellow"))
   562→    print(colorize("  Fix the reflect blueprint before running organize.", "dim"))
   563→    return False, cited, missing, duplicates
   564→
   565→
   566→def _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages: dict) -> bool:
   567→    return require_stage_prerequisite(
   568→        stages,
   569→        flow="organize",
   570→        messages={
   571→            "observe": (
   572→                "  Cannot organize: observe stage not complete.",
   573→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."',
   574→            ),
   575→            "reflect": (
   576→                "  Cannot organize: reflect stage not complete.",
   577→                '  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."',
   578→            ),
   579→        },
   580→    )
   581→
   582→
   583→def _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   584→    *,
   585→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   586→    plan: dict,
   587→    stages: dict,
   588→    attestation: str | None,
   589→    deps: ReflectAutoConfirmDeps | None = None,
   590→) -> bool:
   591→    reflect_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   592→    if reflect_stage is None:
   593→        return False
   594→
   595→    deps = deps or ReflectAutoConfirmDeps()
   596→    resolved_triage_input = deps.triage_input
   597→    if resolved_triage_input is None:
   598→        runtime_factory = deps.command_runtime_fn or command_runtime
   599→        runtime = runtime_factory(args)
   600→        resolved_triage_input = deps.collect_triage_input_fn(plan, runtime.state)
   601→
   602→    valid_ids = set(resolved_triage_input.open_issues.keys())
   603→    accounting_ok, cited_ids, missing_ids, duplicate_ids = _validate_reflect_issue_accounting(
   604→        report=str(reflect_stage.get("report", "")),
   605→        valid_ids=valid_ids,
   606→    )
   607→    if not accounting_ok:
   608→        return False
   609→    reflect_stage["cited_ids"] = sorted(cited_ids)
   610→    reflect_stage["missing_issue_ids"] = missing_ids
   611→    reflect_stage["duplicate_issue_ids"] = duplicate_ids
   612→
   613→    recurring = deps.detect_recurring_patterns_fn(
   614→        resolved_triage_input.open_issues,
   615→        resolved_triage_input.resolved_issues,
   616→    )
   617→    _by_dim, observe_dims = observe_dimension_breakdown(resolved_triage_input)
   618→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   619→    reflect_clusters = [name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")]
   620→    return _auto_confirm_stage(
   621→        plan=plan,
   622→        stage_record=reflect_stage,
   623→        attestation=attestation,
   624→        request=AutoConfirmStageRequest(
   625→            stage_name="reflect",
   626→            stage_label="Reflect",
   627→            blocked_heading="Cannot organize: reflect stage not confirmed.",
   628→            confirm_cmd="desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect",
   629→            inline_hint="Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm reflect inline.",
   630→            dimensions=reflect_dims,
   631→            cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   632→        ),
   633→        save_plan_fn=deps.save_plan_fn,
   634→    )
   635→
   636→
   637→def _manual_clusters_or_error(
   638→    plan: dict,
   639→    *,
   640→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   641→) -> list[str] | None:
   642→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   643→    if manual_clusters:
   644→        return manual_clusters
   645→    if open_review_ids is not None and not open_review_ids:
   646→        return []
   647→    any_clusters = [
   648→        name for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   649→        if cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
   650→    ]
   651→    if any_clusters:
   652→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: only auto-clusters exist.", "red"))
   653→        print(colorize("  Create manual clusters that group issues by root cause:", "dim"))
   654→    else:
   655→        print(colorize("  Cannot organize: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   656→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   657→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
   658→    return None
   659→
   660→
   661→def _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan: dict) -> bool:
   662→    gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
   663→    if not gaps:
   664→        return True
   665→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment.", "red"))
   666→    for name, missing in gaps:
   667→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   668→    print()
   669→    print(colorize("  Each cluster needs a description and action steps:", "dim"))
   670→    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "what this cluster addresses" --steps "step 1" "step 2"', "dim"))
   671→    return False
   672→
   673→
   674→def _unclustered_review_issues_or_error(plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   675→    """Block if open review issues aren't in any manual cluster. Return True if OK."""
   676→    unclustered = unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   677→    if not unclustered:
   678→        return True
   679→    print(colorize(f"  Cannot organize: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no cluster.", "red"))
   680→    for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   681→        short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   682→        print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   683→    if len(unclustered) > 10:
   684→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   685→    print()
   686→    print(colorize("  Every review issue needs an action plan. Either:", "dim"))
   687→    print(colorize("    1. Add to a cluster: desloppify plan cluster add <name> <pattern>", "dim"))
   688→    print(colorize('    2. Wontfix it: desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "reason" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   689→    return False
   690→
   691→
   692→def _organize_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   693→    if not report:
   694→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage organize.", "red"))
   695→        print(colorize("  Summarize your prioritized organization:", "dim"))
   696→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory issues before clustering?", "dim"))
   697→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   698→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   699→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   700→        return None
   701→    if len(report) < 100:
   702→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   703→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   704→        return None
   705→    return report
   706→
   707→
   708→@dataclass(frozen=True)
   709→class LedgerMismatch:
   710→    """One issue where plan state diverges from the reflect disposition."""
   711→
   712→    intended: ReflectDisposition
   713→    actual: ActualDisposition
   714→
   715→    @property
   716→    def issue_id(self) -> str:
   717→        return self.intended.issue_id
   718→
   719→    @property
   720→    def expected_decision(self) -> str:
   721→        return self.intended.decision
   722→
   723→    @property
   724→    def expected_target(self) -> str:
   725→        return self.intended.target
   726→
   727→    @property
   728→    def actual_state(self) -> str:
   729→        return self.actual.describe(self.intended)
   730→
   731→
   732→def _disposition_matches(intended: ReflectDisposition, actual: ActualDisposition) -> bool:
   733→    """True when the actual plan state satisfies the intended disposition."""
   734→    if intended.decision == "permanent_skip":
   735→        return actual.kind == "skipped"
   736→    if intended.decision == "cluster":
   737→        return actual.kind == "clustered" and actual.cluster_name == intended.target
   738→    return False
   739→
   740→
   741→def validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   742→    *,
   743→    plan: dict,
   744→    stages: dict,
   745→) -> list[LedgerMismatch]:
   746→    """Check that plan mutations match the reflect disposition ledger.
   747→
   748→    Returns an empty list when all dispositions are faithfully materialized,
   749→    or a list of mismatches when organize diverged from the reflect plan.
   750→
   751→    Silently returns [] for legacy runs without a disposition_ledger.
   752→    """
   753→    raw_ledger = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("disposition_ledger")
   754→    if not raw_ledger:
   755→        return []
   756→
   757→    ledger = [ReflectDisposition.from_dict(d) for d in raw_ledger]
   758→    actuals = _build_actual_disposition_index(plan)
   759→    unplaced = ActualDisposition(kind="unplaced")
   760→
   761→    return [
   762→        LedgerMismatch(intended=d, actual=actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   763→        for d in ledger
   764→        if not _disposition_matches(d, actuals.get(d.issue_id, unplaced))
   765→    ]
   766→
   767→
   768→def _validate_organize_against_ledger_or_error(
   769→    *,
   770→    plan: dict,
   771→    stages: dict,
   772→) -> bool:
   773→    """Block organize if plan state diverges from the reflect disposition ledger."""
   774→    mismatches = validate_organize_against_reflect_ledger(
   775→        plan=plan, stages=stages,
   776→    )
   777→    if not mismatches:
   778→        return True
   779→
   780→    print(
   781→        colorize(
   782→            f"  Cannot organize: {len(mismatches)} issue(s) diverge from the reflect plan.",
   783→            "red",
   784→        )
   785→    )
   786→    for m in mismatches[:10]:
   787→        short_id = m.issue_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
   788→        print(
   789→            colorize(
   790→                f"    {short_id}: reflect said {m.expected_decision} "
   791→                f'"{m.expected_target}", but {m.actual_state}',
   792→                "yellow",
   793→            )
   794→        )
   795→    if len(mismatches) > 10:
   796→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(mismatches) - 10} more", "yellow"))
   797→    print()
   798→    print(
   799→        colorize(
   800→            "  Fix the plan to match the reflect ledger, or re-run reflect to update dispositions.",
   801→            "dim",
   802→        )
   803→    )
   804→    return False
   805→
   806→
   807→__all__ = [
   808→    "_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete",
   809→    "_auto_confirm_observe_if_attested",
   810→    "AutoConfirmStageRequest",
   811→    "_auto_confirm_stage_for_complete",
```

```
1→"""Sense-check stage command flow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass

     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→
    12→from .records import record_sense_check_stage, resolve_reusable_report
    13→from ..validation.enrich_quality import evaluate_enrich_quality
    14→from ..validation.enrich_checks import (
    15→    _steps_missing_issue_refs,
    16→    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    17→    _steps_with_vague_detail,
    18→    _steps_without_effort,
    19→    _underspecified_steps,
    20→)
    21→from ..helpers import has_triage_in_queue, open_review_ids_from_state, print_cascade_clear_feedback
    22→from ..services import TriageServices, default_triage_services
    23→from .enrich import ColorizeFn
    24→
```
