    26→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    27→class SenseCheckStageDeps:
    28→    has_triage_in_queue: Callable[[dict], bool] = has_triage_in_queue
    29→    resolve_reusable_report: Callable[[str | None, dict | None], tuple[str | None, bool]] = (
    30→        resolve_reusable_report
    31→    )
    32→    record_sense_check_stage: Callable[..., list[str]] = record_sense_check_stage
    33→    colorize: ColorizeFn = colorize
    34→    default_triage_services: Callable[[], TriageServices] = default_triage_services
    35→    print_cascade_clear_feedback: Callable[[list[str], dict], None] = print_cascade_clear_feedback
    36→    get_project_root: Callable[[], Path] | None = None
    37→    underspecified_steps: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _underspecified_steps
    38→    steps_missing_issue_refs: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _steps_missing_issue_refs
    39→    steps_with_bad_paths: Callable[[dict, Path], list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]] = _steps_with_bad_paths
    40→    steps_with_vague_detail: Callable[[dict, Path], list[tuple[str, int, str]]] = _steps_with_vague_detail
    41→    steps_without_effort: Callable[[dict], list[tuple[str, int, int]]] = _steps_without_effort
    42→
    43→
    44→def _sense_check_report(
    45→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    46→    stages: dict,
    47→    *,
    48→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps,
    49→) -> tuple[str | None, bool, dict | None]:
    50→    existing_stage = stages.get("sense-check")
    51→    report = getattr(args, "report", None)
    52→    report, is_reuse = deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    53→    return report, is_reuse, existing_stage
    54→
    55→
    56→def _sense_check_quality_problems(
    57→    plan: dict,
    58→    *,
    59→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps,
    60→) -> list[str]:
    61→    get_project_root = deps.get_project_root
    62→    if get_project_root is None:
    63→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    64→
    65→    repo_root = get_project_root()
    66→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
    67→        plan,
    68→        repo_root,
    69→        phase_label="sense-check",
    70→        bad_paths_severity="failure",
    71→        missing_effort_severity="failure",
    72→        include_missing_issue_refs=True,
    73→        include_vague_detail=True,
    74→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
    75→    )
    76→    return [issue.message for issue in quality_report.failures]
    77→
    78→
    79→def _print_sense_check_problems(
    80→    problems: list[str],
    81→    *,
    82→    colorize_fn: ColorizeFn,
    83→) -> None:
    84→    print(colorize_fn("  Cannot record sense-check — plan still has issues:", "red"))
    85→    for problem in problems:
    86→        print(colorize_fn(f"    • {problem}", "yellow"))
    87→    print(colorize_fn("  Fix these before recording the sense-check stage.", "dim"))
    88→
    89→
    90→def _report_length_ok(report: str | None, *, colorize_fn: ColorizeFn) -> bool:
    91→    if not report:
    92→        print(colorize_fn("  --report is required for --stage sense-check.", "red"))
    93→        print(
    94→            colorize_fn(
    95→                "  Describe what the content and structure subagents found and fixed.",
    96→                "dim",
    97→            )
    98→        )
    99→        return False
   100→    if len(report) < 100:
   101→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   102→        return False
   103→    return True
   104→
   105→
   106→def _sense_check_evidence_failures(
   107→    report: str,
   108→    *,
   109→    plan: dict,
   110→    state: dict,
   111→) -> tuple[list[object], list[object]]:
   112→    from .evidence_parsing import (
   113→        validate_report_has_file_paths,
   114→        validate_report_references_clusters,
   115→    )
   116→    from ..helpers import manual_clusters_with_issues
   117→
   118→    failures: list[object] = []
   119→    if open_review_ids_from_state(state):
   120→        failures.extend(validate_report_has_file_paths(report) or [])
   121→
   122→    cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   123→    if cluster_names:
   124→        failures.extend(validate_report_references_clusters(report, cluster_names) or [])
   125→
   126→    blocking = [failure for failure in failures if failure.blocking]
   127→    advisory = [failure for failure in failures if not failure.blocking]
   128→    return blocking, advisory
   129→
   130→
   131→def _print_evidence_failures(
   132→    failures: list[object],
   133→    *,
   134→    stage_label: str,
   135→    colorize_fn: ColorizeFn,
   136→    style: str,
   137→) -> None:
   138→    from .evidence_parsing import format_evidence_failures
   139→
   140→    if not failures:
   141→        return
   142→    print(colorize_fn(format_evidence_failures(failures, stage_label=stage_label), style))
   143→
   144→
   145→def run_stage_sense_check(
   146→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   147→    *,
   148→    services: TriageServices | None,
   149→    deps: SenseCheckStageDeps | None = None,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    """Record the SENSE-CHECK stage after rerunning enrich-level validations."""
   152→    resolved_deps = deps or SenseCheckStageDeps()
   153→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   154→
   155→    resolved_services = services or resolved_deps.default_triage_services()
   156→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   157→    state = resolved_services.command_runtime(args).state
   158→
   159→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   160→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to sense-check.", "yellow"))
   161→        return
   162→
   163→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   164→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   165→    report, is_reuse, existing_stage = _sense_check_report(
   166→        args,
   167→        stages,
   168→        deps=resolved_deps,
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    if not stages.get("enrich", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   172→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Cannot sense-check: enrich stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   173→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    problems = _sense_check_quality_problems(plan, deps=resolved_deps)
   177→    if problems:
   178→        _print_sense_check_problems(problems, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize)
   179→        return
   180→
   181→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All enrich-level checks pass after sense-check.", "green"))
   182→
   183→    if not _report_length_ok(report, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize):
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    blocking_ev, advisory_ev = _sense_check_evidence_failures(report, plan=plan, state=state)
   187→    if blocking_ev:
   188→        _print_evidence_failures(
   189→            blocking_ev,
   190→            stage_label="sense-check",
   191→            colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize,
   192→            style="red",
   193→        )
   194→        return
   195→    if advisory_ev:
   196→        _print_evidence_failures(
   197→            advisory_ev,
   198→            stage_label="sense-check",
   199→            colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize,
   200→            style="yellow",
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   204→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_sense_check_stage(
   205→        stages,
   206→        report=report,
   207→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   208→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   209→    )
   210→
   211→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   212→
   213→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   214→        plan,
   215→        "triage_sense_check",
   216→        actor="user",
   217→        detail={"reuse": is_reuse},
   218→    )
   219→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   220→
   221→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Sense-check stage recorded.", "green"))
   222→    if is_reuse:
   223→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Sense-check data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   224→        if cleared:
   225→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   226→    else:
   227→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the sense-check.", "yellow"))
   228→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm sense-check", "dim"))
   229→
   230→
   231→def cmd_stage_sense_check(
   232→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   233→    *,
   234→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   235→) -> None:
   236→    """Public entrypoint for sense-check stage recording."""
   237→    run_stage_sense_check(args, services=services)
   238→
   239→
   240→__all__ = [
   241→    "SenseCheckStageDeps",
   242→    "cmd_stage_sense_check",
   243→    "record_sense_check_stage",
   244→    "run_stage_sense_check",
   245→]
   246→

    54→def _resolve_enrich_stage_context(

    56→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    57→    services: TriageServices | None,
    58→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
    59→) -> tuple[TriageServices, dict, dict, dict, str | None, str | None]:

    62→    resolved_services = services or deps.default_triage_services()

    65→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})

    99→
   100→def _require_cluster_update_activity(
   101→    *,
   102→    plan: dict,
   103→    state: dict,
   104→    stages: dict,
   105→    attestation: str | None,
   106→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   107→) -> bool:
   108→    organize_ts = stages.get("organize", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   109→    if not organize_ts:
   110→        return True
   111→    activity = deps.count_log_activity_since(plan, organize_ts)
   112→    update_ops = activity.get("cluster_update", 0)
   113→    if update_ops != 0 or not open_review_ids_from_state(state):
   114→        return True
   115→    if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= 40:
   116→        print(
   117→            deps.colorize(
   118→                "  Note: 0 cluster_update ops logged since organize. "
   119→                "Proceeding with attestation override.",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        return True
   124→    print(
   125→        deps.colorize(
   126→            "  Cannot enrich: no cluster_update operations logged since organize.",
   127→            "red",
   128→        )
   129→    )
   130→    print(
   131→        deps.colorize(
   132→            "  Enriching steps requires running cluster update commands.\n"
   133→            '  e.g. desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..."',
   134→            "dim",
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→    print(
   138→        deps.colorize(
   139→            '  Override: pass --attestation "reason why no update ops" (40+ chars).',
   140→            "dim",
   141→        )
   142→    )
   143→    return False
   144→
   145→
   146→def _print_underspecified_step_error(
   147→    underspec: list[tuple[str, int, int]],
   148→    *,
   149→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in underspec)
   152→    print(
   153→        deps.colorize(
   154→            f"  Cannot enrich: {total_bare} step(s) across {len(underspec)} cluster(s) lack detail or issue_refs:",
   155→            "red",
   156→        )
   157→    )
   158→    for name, bare, total in underspec:
   159→        print(deps.colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   160→    print()
   161→    print(
   162→        deps.colorize(
   163→            "  Every step needs --detail (sub-points) or --issue-refs (for auto-completion).",
   164→            "dim",
   165→        )
   166→    )
   167→    print(deps.colorize("  Fix:", "dim"))
   168→    print(
   169→        deps.colorize(
   170→            '    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
   171→            "dim",
   172→        )
   173→    )
   174→    print(
   175→        deps.colorize(
   176→            "  You can also still reorganize: add/remove clusters, reorder, etc.",
   177→            "dim",
   178→        )
   179→    )
   180→
   181→
   182→def _print_enrich_warnings(
   183→    *,
   184→    report,
   185→    deps: EnrichStageDeps,
   186→) -> None:
   187→    bad_paths = report.warning("bad_paths")
   188→    if bad_paths:
   189→        print(
   190→            deps.colorize(
   191→                f"  Warning: {bad_paths.total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk:",
   192→                "yellow",
   193→            )
   194→        )
   195→        for name, step_num, paths in bad_paths.rows[:5]:
   196→            print(deps.colorize(f"    {name} step {step_num}: {', '.join(paths[:3])}", "yellow"))
   197→        print(
   198→            deps.colorize(
   199→                "  Fix paths before confirming enrich (confirmation will block on bad paths).",
   200→                "dim",
   201→            )
   202→        )
   203→
   204→    untagged = report.warning("missing_effort")
   205→    if untagged:
   206→        print(deps.colorize(f"  Note: {untagged.total} step(s) have no effort tag.", "yellow"))
   207→        print(
   208→            deps.colorize(
   209→                "  Consider: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --effort small",
   210→                "dim",
   211→            )
   212→        )
   213→
   214→
   215→def run_stage_enrich(
   216→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   217→    *,
   218→    services: TriageServices | None,
   219→    deps: EnrichStageDeps | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Record the ENRICH stage with validation and optional auto-confirm."""
   222→    resolved_deps = deps or EnrichStageDeps()
   223→    resolved_services, plan, state, stages, report, attestation = _resolve_enrich_stage_context(
   224→        args=args,
   225→        services=services,
   226→        deps=resolved_deps,
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    if not resolved_deps.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   230→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to enrich.", "yellow"))
   231→        return
   232→
   233→    existing_stage = stages.get("enrich")
   234→    report, is_reuse = resolved_deps.resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
   235→
   236→    if not resolved_deps.require_organize_stage_for_enrich(stages):
   237→        return
   238→
   239→    if not _require_confirmed_organize_stage(
   240→        plan=plan,
   241→        stages=stages,
   242→        attestation=attestation,
   243→        services=resolved_services,
   244→        deps=resolved_deps,
   245→    ):
   246→        return
   247→
   248→    if not is_reuse and not _require_cluster_update_activity(
   249→        plan=plan,
   250→        state=state,
   251→        stages=stages,
   252→        attestation=attestation,
   253→        deps=resolved_deps,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    get_project_root = resolved_deps.get_project_root
   258→    if get_project_root is None:
   259→        from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
   260→
   261→    quality_report = evaluate_enrich_quality(
   262→        plan,
   263→        get_project_root(),
   264→        phase_label="enrich",
   265→        bad_paths_severity="warning",
   266→        missing_effort_severity="warning",
   267→        include_missing_issue_refs=False,
   268→        include_vague_detail=False,
   269→        stale_issue_refs_severity=None,
   270→    )
   271→    underspec = quality_report.failure("underspecified")
   272→    total_bare = underspec.total if underspec else 0
   273→
   274→    if underspec:
   275→        _print_underspecified_step_error(underspec.rows, deps=resolved_deps)
   276→        return
   277→
   278→    print(resolved_deps.colorize("  All steps have detail or issue_refs.", "green"))
   279→    _print_enrich_warnings(report=quality_report, deps=resolved_deps)
   280→
   281→    report = resolved_deps.enrich_report_or_error(report)
   282→    if report is None:
   283→        return
   284→
   285→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   286→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   287→    cleared = resolved_deps.record_enrich_stage(
   288→        stages,
   289→        report=report,
   290→        shallow_count=total_bare,
   291→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   292→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   293→    )
   294→
   295→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   296→
   297→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   298→        plan,
   299→        "triage_enrich",
   300→        actor="user",
   301→        detail={"shallow_count": total_bare, "reuse": is_reuse},
   302→    )
   303→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   304→
   305→    print(
   306→        resolved_deps.colorize(
   307→            f"  Enrich stage recorded: {total_bare} step(s) still without detail.",
   308→            "green",
   309→        )
   310→    )
   311→    if is_reuse:
   312→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Enrich data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   313→        if cleared:
   314→            resolved_deps.print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   315→    else:
   316→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("  Now confirm the enrichment.", "yellow"))
   317→        print(resolved_deps.colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   318→
   319→    resolved_deps.print_user_message(
   320→        "Enrich recorded. Before confirming — check the subagent's"
   321→        " work. Could a developer who has never seen this code"
   322→        " execute every step without asking a question? Every step"
   323→        " needs: file path, specific location, specific action."
   324→        " 'Refactor X' fails. 'Extract lines 45-89 into Y' passes."
   325→    )
   326→
   327→
   328→def cmd_stage_enrich(
   329→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   330→    *,
   331→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   332→) -> None:
   333→    """Public entrypoint for enrich stage recording."""
   334→    run_stage_enrich(args, services=services)
   335→
   336→
   337→__all__ = ["ColorizeFn", "EnrichStageDeps", "cmd_stage_enrich", "run_stage_enrich"]
   338→
```

1→"""Triage/commit-log/scan-gate parser section builders for plan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import argparse

     8→def _add_triage_subparser(plan_sub) -> None:
     9→    p_triage = plan_sub.add_parser(
    10→        "triage",
    11→        help="Staged triage workflow for review issues",
    12→        epilog="""\
    13→examples:
    14→  desloppify plan triage
    15→  desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner codex
    16→  desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner claude
    17→  desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner codex --only-stages organize
    18→  desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."   # manual fallback
    19→  desloppify plan triage --confirm-existing --note "..." --strategy "same" --confirmed "I reviewed the new issues and the existing plan still holds."\
    20→""",
    21→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    22→    )
    23→    p_triage.add_argument(
    24→        "--stage",
    25→        type=str,
    26→        choices=["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"],
    27→        default=None,
    28→        help="Stage to record",
    29→    )
    30→    p_triage.add_argument(
    31→        "--report", type=str, default=None,
    32→        help="Stage report text",
    33→    )
    34→    p_triage.add_argument(
    35→        "--complete", action="store_true", default=False,

    37→    )
    38→    p_triage.add_argument(
    39→        "--strategy", type=str, default=None,
    40→        help="Strategy summary (for --complete)",
    41→    )
    42→    p_triage.add_argument(
    43→        "--confirm-existing", action="store_true", default=False,
    44→        help="Fast-track confirmation of existing plan",
    45→    )
    46→    p_triage.add_argument(
    47→        "--note", type=str, default=None,
    48→        help="Note for --confirm-existing",
    49→    )
    50→    p_triage.add_argument(
    51→        "--start", action="store_true", default=False,
    52→        help="Manually start triage (inject triage stages, clear prior stages)",
    53→    )
    54→    p_triage.add_argument(
    55→        "--confirm",
    56→        type=str,

   106→def _add_commit_log_subparser(plan_sub) -> None:
   107→    p_commit_log = plan_sub.add_parser(
   108→        "commit-log",
   109→        help="Track commits and resolved issues for PR updates",
   110→        epilog="""\
   111→examples:
   112→  desloppify plan commit-log                     # show status
   113→  desloppify plan commit-log record              # record HEAD commit
   114→  desloppify plan commit-log record --note "..."  # with rationale
   115→  desloppify plan commit-log record --only "smells::*"
   116→  desloppify plan commit-log history             # show commit records
   117→  desloppify plan commit-log pr                  # print PR body markdown""",
   118→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   119→    )
   120→    commit_log_sub = p_commit_log.add_subparsers(dest="commit_log_action")
   121→
   122→    p_cl_record = commit_log_sub.add_parser("record", help="Record a commit with resolved issues")
   123→    p_cl_record.add_argument("--sha", type=str, default=None, help="Commit SHA (default: auto-detect HEAD)")
   124→    p_cl_record.add_argument("--branch", type=str, default=None, help="Branch name (default: auto-detect)")
   125→    p_cl_record.add_argument("--note", type=str, default=None, help="Commit rationale/description")
   126→    p_cl_record.add_argument("--only", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN", default=None, help="Record only matching issues (glob patterns)")
   127→
   128→    p_cl_history = commit_log_sub.add_parser("history", help="Show commit records")
   129→    p_cl_history.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=10, help="Number of records to show (default: 10)")
   130→
   131→    commit_log_sub.add_parser("pr", help="Print PR body markdown (dry run)")
   132→
   133→
   134→def _add_scan_gate_subparser(plan_sub) -> None:
   135→    p_sg = plan_sub.add_parser(
   136→        "scan-gate",
   137→        help="Check or skip the scan requirement for workflow items",
   138→        epilog="""\
   139→examples:
   140→  desloppify plan scan-gate                        # check scan gate status
   141→  desloppify plan scan-gate --skip --note "..."    # mark scan requirement as satisfied""",
   142→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   143→    )
   144→    p_sg.add_argument(
   145→        "--skip", action="store_true", default=False,
   146→        help="Mark the scan requirement as satisfied without running a scan",
   147→    )
   148→    p_sg.add_argument(
   149→        "--note", type=str, default=None,
   150→        help="Explanation for skipping (required with --skip, min 50 chars)",
   151→    )
   152→
   153→
   154→def _add_policy_subparser(plan_sub) -> None:
   155→    p_policy = plan_sub.add_parser(
   156→        "policy",
   157→        help="Manage project policy rules (enforced during triage and review)",
   158→        epilog="""\
   159→examples:
   160→  desloppify plan policy                            # list rules
   161→  desloppify plan policy add "No re-export facades" # add a rule
   162→  desloppify plan policy remove 2                   # remove rule #2""",
   163→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   164→    )
   165→    policy_sub = p_policy.add_subparsers(dest="policy_action")
   166→    p_add = policy_sub.add_parser("add", help="Add a policy rule")
   167→    p_add.add_argument("rule_text", type=str, help="Rule text")
   168→    p_remove = policy_sub.add_parser("remove", help="Remove a policy rule by number")
   169→    p_remove.add_argument("rule_index", type=int, help="Rule number to remove")
   170→
   171→
   172→def _add_repair_state_subparser(plan_sub) -> None:
   173→    plan_sub.add_parser(
   174→        "repair-state",
   175→        help="Rebuild state.json from surviving plan metadata",
   176→        epilog="""\
   177→examples:
   178→  desloppify plan repair-state
   179→  desloppify plan repair-state --state .desloppify/state-typescript.json""",
   180→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   181→    )
   182→
   183→
   184→__all__ = [
   185→    "_add_commit_log_subparser",
   186→    "_add_policy_subparser",
   187→    "_add_repair_state_subparser",
   188→    "_add_scan_gate_subparser",
   189→    "_add_triage_subparser",
   190→]
   191→

1→"""Stage-gate and coverage helpers for triage command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import TRIAGE_IDS
     7→
     8→from ..helpers import cluster_issue_ids, live_active_triage_issue_ids
     9→
    10→
    11→def _require_triage_pending(plan: dict, *, action: str) -> bool:
    12→    """Require at least one triage stage ID to be present in queue for an action."""
    13→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    14→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
    15→        return True
    16→    print(colorize(f"  No triage stage in the queue — nothing to {action}.", "yellow"))
    17→    return False
    18→
    19→
    20→def _validate_stage_report(
    21→    report: str | None,
    22→    *,
    23→    stage: str,
    24→    min_chars: int,
    25→    missing_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    26→    short_guidance: list[str] | None = None,
    27→) -> str | None:
    28→    """Validate staged report presence/length and print consistent guidance."""
    29→    if not report:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  --report is required for --stage {stage}.", "red"))
    31→        for line in missing_guidance or []:
    32→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    33→        return None
    34→    cleaned = report.strip()
    35→    if len(cleaned) < min_chars:
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                f"  Report too short: {len(cleaned)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).",
    39→                "red",
    40→            )
    41→        )
    42→        for line in short_guidance or []:
    43→            print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
    44→        return None
    45→    return cleaned
    46→
    47→
    48→def unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    49→    """Return clusters with issues that are missing required enrichment.
    50→
    51→    Requirements:
    52→    - Every cluster needs a description and at least one action_step.
    53→    - Small clusters (< 5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue,
    54→      so each item has a concrete plan. Large clusters (>= 5) just need
    55→      steps overall (cluster-level plan is sufficient).
    56→    """
    57→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    58→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    59→        issue_ids = cluster_issue_ids(cluster)
    60→        if not issue_ids:
    61→            continue
    62→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    63→            continue
    64→        missing: list[str] = []
    65→        if not cluster.get("description"):
    66→            missing.append("description")
    67→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    68→        issue_count = len(issue_ids)
    69→        if not steps:
    70→            missing.append("action_steps")
    71→        elif issue_count < 5 and len(steps) < issue_count:
    72→            missing.append(
    73→                f"action_steps (have {len(steps)}, need >= {issue_count} for small cluster)"
    74→            )
    75→        if missing:
    76→            gaps.append((name, missing))
    77→    return gaps
    78→
    79→
    80→def unclustered_review_issues(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
    81→    """Return review issue IDs that aren't in any manual cluster.
    82→
    83→    When *state* is provided, uses open review/concerns issues from state
    84→    (the canonical source). Falls back to scanning queue_order for backwards
    85→    compatibility.
    86→    """
    87→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    88→    clustered_ids: set[str] = set()
    89→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    90→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
    91→            clustered_ids.update(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
    92→    skipped_ids = {
    93→        fid for fid in (plan.get("skipped", {}) or {}).keys() if isinstance(fid, str)
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    if state is not None:
    97→        review_ids = [
    98→            fid for fid, finding in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    99→            if finding.get("status") == "open"
   100→            and finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   101→        ]
   102→        frozen_ids = (plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}) or {}).get("active_triage_issue_ids")
   103→        if isinstance(frozen_ids, list) and frozen_ids:
   104→            frozen_id_set = live_active_triage_issue_ids(plan, state)
   105→            review_ids = [fid for fid in review_ids if fid in frozen_id_set]
   106→    else:
   107→        review_ids = [
   108→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   109→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   110→            and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   111→        ]
   112→
   113→    return [
   114→        fid for fid in review_ids
   115→        if fid not in clustered_ids and fid not in skipped_ids
   116→    ]
   117→
```

The three validators are NOT unused -- they are used in `stage_validation.py` (runner) and `confirmations/organize.py` as advisory warnings. The user's assessment was wrong on this point.

Now I have enough information to design the plan. Let me summarize my findings:

**`_underspecified_steps` triple-call sites:**
1. `enrich_quality.py` line 91 (via `evaluate_enrich_quality`)
2. `completion_stages.py` line 84 (`_require_enrich_stage_for_complete`)
3. `completion_stages.py` line 109 (`_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete`)

The enrich stage calls `evaluate_enrich_quality` which calls `_underspecified_steps` once. The sense-check stage also calls `evaluate_enrich_quality` which calls it once. But during completion flow, `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete` calls it directly, and `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete` calls it directly too, so the completion path can call it 2 times independently.

**Scoping issue**: `_manual_clusters_with_issues` in `enrich_checks.py` iterates ALL manual clusters, with no filtering by current triage cycle's `active_triage_issue_ids`.

Here is the implementation plan:

---

## Implementation Plan: Triage Validation Friction Reduction (Issues 397, 398, 399)

### Overview

Six focused changes across 7 files. The core insight: path validation must be smart enough to handle executor-ready step descriptions (which reference future paths and line-number suffixes), but should never block completion. Content quality checks (detail length, issue_refs, effort tags) remain blocking.

### Change 1: Fix the path regex and extraction logic

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/enrich_checks.py`

**Problem**: `_PATH_RE` only matches paths containing `src/` or `supabase/`. It does not strip line-number suffixes (`:45`), backtick wrapping, or recognize creation/move intent.

**Changes**:
1. Update `_PATH_RE` to also strip trailing `:digits` (line number suffixes): change `\.\w+` to `\.\w+(?::\d+)?` and strip the suffix before checking existence.
2. In `_detail_paths()`, strip backticks from matched paths.
3. In `_steps_with_bad_paths()`, add heuristics to skip paths that are clearly creation destinations:
   - If the step detail contains "create", "move to", "rename to", "new file", "extract to", or "generate" before the path reference, skip that path from validation.
   - This is a simple keyword check on the detail text surrounding the path match.
4. In `_path_exists_or_alt_exists()`, strip any trailing `:\d+` before checking existence.

**Specific implementation**:
```python
_PATH_RE = re.compile(r"(?:[\w./-]+/)?(?:src|supabase)/[\w./-]+\.\w+(?::\d+)?")
_CREATION_KEYWORDS = re.compile(r"\b(?:create|move\s+to|rename\s+to|new\s+file|extract\s+(?:to|into)|generate)\b", re.IGNORECASE)

def _detail_paths(detail: str) -> list[str]:
    """Extract file paths from one step detail string."""
    if not detail:
        return []
    return [p.strip("`").split(":")[0] if ":" in p.split("/")[-1] else p.strip("`")
            for p in _PATH_RE.findall(detail)]
```

Actually, simpler approach -- keep `_detail_paths` returning raw matches, and strip the line suffix in `_path_exists_or_alt_exists`. Add a new helper `_is_creation_context` that checks if the path appears in a creation context in the detail string. Then in `_steps_with_bad_paths`, skip paths in creation context.

### Change 2: Make path validation always advisory (warning severity)

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/sense_check.py`

**Problem**: `_sense_check_quality_problems()` calls `evaluate_enrich_quality` with `bad_paths_severity="failure"`, making bad paths block sense-check completion.

**Change**: Change `bad_paths_severity="failure"` to `bad_paths_severity="warning"` on line 70 of `sense_check.py`.

This is a one-line change. The enrich stage already uses `"warning"` (line 265 of `enrich.py`). After this, path validation is advisory everywhere.

Additionally, print warnings in the sense-check flow so the user sees them but is not blocked. Currently `_sense_check_quality_problems` only returns `failures` (line 76). Add a parallel advisory output for warnings.

### Change 3: Scope sense-check/enrich validation to current triage cycle clusters

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/enrich_checks.py`

**Problem**: `_manual_clusters_with_issues()` iterates ALL manual clusters. Should only validate clusters whose issue_ids overlap with the current triage cycle's `active_triage_issue_ids`.

**Change**: Add an optional `triage_issue_ids: set[str] | None` parameter to `_manual_clusters_with_issues()`. When provided, only yield clusters that have at least one issue_id in the set. All existing callers would need to pass this.

However, this is invasive -- it changes the signature of a function used by 9 other functions. Better approach: add `triage_issue_ids` as an optional parameter to `evaluate_enrich_quality()` in `enrich_quality.py`, and pass it through. The individual check functions (`_underspecified_steps`, `_steps_with_bad_paths`, etc.) would gain an optional `cluster_filter: set[str] | None` parameter, or more practically, filter the plan before passing it in.

**Simplest approach**: Add a `_filter_plan_to_cycle` helper that returns a shallow copy of the plan with only clusters whose issues overlap the triage cycle set. Call it at the top of `evaluate_enrich_quality` and in the completion_stages direct calls. This avoids changing signatures of 9+ functions.

```python
def _filter_plan_to_triage_cycle(plan: dict, triage_issue_ids: set[str] | None) -> dict:
    if triage_issue_ids is None:
        return plan
    filtered_clusters = {}
    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
        if cluster.get("auto"):
            continue
        ids = set(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
        if ids & triage_issue_ids:
            filtered_clusters[name] = cluster
    return {**plan, "clusters": filtered_clusters}
```

**Callers to update**:
- `evaluate_enrich_quality()` in `enrich_quality.py` -- add `triage_issue_ids` param, filter at top
- `_sense_check_quality_problems()` in `sense_check.py` -- pass triage_issue_ids from plan's `active_triage_issue_ids`
- `run_stage_enrich()` in `enrich.py` -- pass triage_issue_ids
- `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete()` and `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete()` in `completion_stages.py` -- pass triage_issue_ids

### Change 4: Add `--report-file` option

**Files**:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_plan_impl_sections_triage_commit_scan.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/helpers.py` or the individual stage files

**Change**: Add `--report-file` argument to the triage subparser. In the stage dispatch, if `--report-file` is set and `--report` is not, read the file contents as the report text. This is a standard pattern.

## Implementation Plan: Triage Validation Friction Reduction (Issues 397, 398, 399)

### Summary

Six changes, ordered by dependency. The three "unused validators" (`_cluster_file_overlaps`, `_clusters_with_directory_scatter`, `_clusters_with_high_step_ratio`) are actually used in `stage_validation.py` and `confirmations/organize.py` as advisory warnings during organize confirmation and runner validation. They should NOT be removed.

### Change 1: Fix path extraction to handle real-world step descriptions

**File**: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/enrich_checks.py`

The `_PATH_RE` regex and `_steps_with_bad_paths` logic need three fixes:

**1a. Handle line-number suffixes.** Modify `_path_exists_or_alt_exists` to strip trailing `:\d+` from `path_str` before constructing the `Path`. The regex itself can stay the same -- the suffix gets captured as part of the extension match (`\.\w+` matches `.ts` and then `:45` is outside), but backtick-wrapped paths like `` `src/foo.ts:45` `` need the backtick stripped first. Actually, looking more carefully at the regex: `\.\w+` matches `.ts` and stops, so `:45` is not captured. The issue is when the path appears as `src/foo.ts:45` without a space -- the regex won't match because `:` is not in `[\w./-]`. So the regex already excludes these. The fix is: make the regex optionally capture `:digits` so these paths get matched at all: change to `r"(?:[\w./-]+/)?(?:src|supabase)/[\w./-]+\.\w+(?::\d+(?:[-:]\d+)*)?"`. Then in `_path_exists_or_alt_exists`, strip the line suffix.

**1b. Skip creation-destination paths.** In `_steps_with_bad_paths`, before marking a path as "bad", check if the surrounding detail text indicates the path is a creation target. Add a simple check: if any of `create`, `move to`, `rename to`, `extract to`, `extract into`, `new file`, `generate` appear in the 60 characters preceding the path match position, skip it.

**1c. Implementation sketch:**

```python
_PATH_RE = re.compile(r"(?:[\w./-]+/)?(?:src|supabase)/[\w./-]+\.\w+(?::\d+(?:[-:]\d+)*)?")
_CREATION_CONTEXT_RE = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:creat|mov(?:e|ing)\s+to|renam(?:e|ing)\s+to|extract(?:ing)?\s+(?:to|into)"
    r"|new\s+file|generat)",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

def _strip_line_suffix(path_str: str) -> str:
    """Remove trailing :line or :line:col from a path string."""
    # Strip backticks first
    cleaned = path_str.strip("`")
    # Remove :digits suffix
    return re.sub(r"(:\d+)+$", "", cleaned)

def _path_exists_or_alt_exists(repo_root: Path, path_str: str) -> bool:
    cleaned = _strip_line_suffix(path_str)
    path = repo_root / cleaned
    if path.exists():
        return True
    alt_ext = _EXT_SWAPS.get(path.suffix)
    return bool(alt_ext and path.with_suffix(alt_ext).exists())

def _is_creation_context(detail: str, path_str: str) -> bool:
    """Check if path appears in a creation/move-destination context."""
    idx = detail.find(path_str)
    if idx < 0:
        return False
    prefix = detail[max(0, idx - 80):idx]
    return bool(_CREATION_CONTEXT_RE.search(prefix))
```

Then in `_steps_with_bad_paths`, filter:
```python
bad = [
    path_str
    for path_str in _detail_paths(detail)
    if not _is_creation_context(detail, path_str)
    and not _path_exists_or_alt_exists(repo_root, path_str)
]
```

### Change 2: Make path validation advisory everywhere

**File**: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/sense_check.py`

Line 70: change `bad_paths_severity="failure"` to `bad_paths_severity="warning"`.

Additionally, update `_sense_check_quality_problems` to also return warnings so they can be printed as advisory info. Currently it only returns `[issue.message for issue in quality_report.failures]`. Add a secondary return or print warnings inline after the quality check passes.

Better approach: after the quality problems check on lines 176-179, add a warning print:

```python
problems = _sense_check_quality_problems(plan, deps=resolved_deps)
if problems:
    _print_sense_check_problems(problems, colorize_fn=resolved_deps.colorize)
    return

# Print advisory warnings (non-blocking)
warnings = _sense_check_quality_warnings(plan, deps=resolved_deps)
if warnings:
    for w in warnings:
        print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"  Advisory: {w}", "yellow"))
```

This requires a new `_sense_check_quality_warnings` function (or modify `_sense_check_quality_problems` to return both). Simplest: make it return the `EnrichQualityReport` directly instead of just failure messages, and let the caller handle both.

### Change 3: Scope validation to current triage cycle clusters

**Files**:
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/enrich_checks.py` -- add `_filter_plan_to_triage_cycle` helper
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/enrich_quality.py` -- add `triage_issue_ids` param to `evaluate_enrich_quality`
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/sense_check.py` -- pass `triage_issue_ids` from `active_triage_issue_ids`
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/enrich.py` -- pass `triage_issue_ids`
- `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/completion_stages.py` -- pass `triage_issue_ids` in direct `_underspecified_steps` calls

The approach: add `_filter_plan_to_triage_cycle(plan, triage_issue_ids)` to `enrich_checks.py`. Add `triage_issue_ids: set[str] | None = None` parameter to `evaluate_enrich_quality`. Filter the plan at the top before passing to check functions. For direct calls in `completion_stages.py`, either call the filter inline or pass filtered plan.

**Getting triage_issue_ids at call sites**: The sense-check and enrich stages both already load `plan`. The triage issue IDs are at `plan["epic_triage_meta"]["active_triage_issue_ids"]` (a list). Convert to `set()` and pass through.

### Change 5: Deduplicate `_underspecified_steps` calls in completion flow

**File**: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/completion_stages.py`

Currently `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete` (line 84) and `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete` (line 109) both call `_underspecified_steps(plan)` independently. These are called in sequence during the completion flow.

The fix: `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete` is only called after `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete` has already passed. But they are independent functions with no shared state. The simplest deduplication: have `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete` accept an optional `pre_checked_underspec: bool = None` parameter. When the caller has already verified no underspecified steps, pass `pre_checked_underspec=True` to skip the redundant check.

Alternatively, since these are called from `completion.py`, refactor the completion flow to call `_underspecified_steps` once and pass the result to both. Let me check the completion flow.

Now I can see the full picture. The completion flow calls `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete` (which calls `_underspecified_steps` on line 84 of completion_stages.py), and then immediately calls `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete` (which calls `_underspecified_steps` again on line 109). These run sequentially and the second call is redundant if the first passed.

## Implementation Plan: Triage Validation Friction Reduction (Issues 397, 398, 399)

### Correction on "unused validators"

The three validators (`_cluster_file_overlaps`, `_clusters_with_directory_scatter`, `_clusters_with_high_step_ratio`) are NOT unused. They are called as advisory warnings in:
- `runner/stage_validation.py` lines 130-137 (`_organize_stage_warnings`)
- `confirmations/organize.py` lines 76-92 (`_print_cluster_shape_warnings`)

These should be left alone. They produce useful advisory output and do not block anything.

### Change 1: Fix path extraction and validation (enrich_checks.py)

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/validation/enrich_checks.py`

Three sub-changes:

**1a.** Update `_PATH_RE` to capture optional line-number suffixes so paths like `src/foo.ts:45` get matched:
```
r"(?:[\w./-]+/)?(?:src|supabase)/[\w./-]+\.\w+(?::\d+(?:[-:]\d+)*)?"
```

**1b.** Add `_strip_line_suffix(path_str)` helper. Update `_path_exists_or_alt_exists` to call it before checking existence.

**1c.** Add `_is_creation_context(detail, path_str)` with a regex for creation keywords (create, move to, rename to, extract to/into, new file, generate) in the 80 chars preceding the path. Update `_steps_with_bad_paths` to skip paths in creation context.

**Tests to update**: `test_triage_tooling_fixes.py` and `test_triage_split_modules_direct.py` will need test cases for the new behavior.

### Change 2: Make path validation advisory at sense-check (sense_check.py)

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/stages/sense_check.py`

Line 70: change `bad_paths_severity="failure"` to `bad_paths_severity="warning"`.

Refactor `_sense_check_quality_problems` to return the full `EnrichQualityReport` instead of just failure messages. Update `run_stage_sense_check` to print warnings as advisory after the blocking check passes:

```python
def _sense_check_quality_report(plan, *, deps):
    # ... same as _sense_check_quality_problems but returns EnrichQualityReport
    return quality_report

# In run_stage_sense_check:
quality_report = _sense_check_quality_report(plan, deps=resolved_deps)
if quality_report.failures:
    _print_sense_check_problems([f.message for f in quality_report.failures], ...)
    return
# Print advisory warnings
for w in quality_report.warnings:
    print(resolved_deps.colorize(f"  Advisory: {w.message}", "yellow"))
```

### Change 3: Scope validation to current triage cycle clusters

**Files**:
- `enrich_checks.py` -- add `_filter_plan_to_triage_cycle(plan, triage_issue_ids)` helper
- `enrich_quality.py` -- add `triage_issue_ids: set[str] | None = None` param to `evaluate_enrich_quality`, filter plan at top
- `sense_check.py` -- extract `active_triage_issue_ids` from plan meta and pass to evaluator
- `enrich.py` -- same
- `completion_stages.py` -- pass filtered plan to `_underspecified_steps` calls

The filter function:
```python
def _filter_plan_to_triage_cycle(plan: dict, triage_issue_ids: set[str] | None) -> dict:
    if triage_issue_ids is None:
        return plan
    filtered = {}
    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
        cids = set(cluster_issue_ids(cluster))
        if cids & triage_issue_ids:
            filtered[name] = cluster
    # Keep auto clusters and non-cluster plan data
    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
        if cluster.get("auto"):
            filtered[name] = cluster
    return {**plan, "clusters": filtered}
```

At call sites, extract the triage IDs:
```python
meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
raw_ids = meta.get("active_triage_issue_ids")
triage_issue_ids = set(raw_ids) if isinstance(raw_ids, list) else None
```

### Change 5: Deduplicate `_underspecified_steps` in completion flow

**File**: `completion_stages.py`

Add `skip_underspec_check: bool = False` parameter to `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete`. When the caller knows `_require_enrich_stage_for_complete` already passed (which means either the enrich stage exists and underspec was checked, or the stage requirement was satisfied), skip the redundant check.

In `completion.py`, the lambda on line 186 passes `skip_underspec_check=True`:
```python
lambda: _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
    plan=plan, stages=stages, attestation=attestation,
    save_plan_fn=resolved_services.save_plan,
    skip_underspec_check=True,
),
```

In `_auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete`, gate the check:
```python
def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(*, plan, stages, attestation, save_plan_fn=None, skip_underspec_check=False):
    if "enrich" not in stages:
        return False
    if not skip_underspec_check:
        underspec = _underspecified_steps(plan)
        if underspec:
            # ... existing error printing
            return False
    return _auto_confirm_stage_for_complete(...)
```

Add a test in the triage test suite that verifies `--report-file` reads from a file and populates `args.report`. Add a test that verifies error handling for missing/unreadable files.

Changes 1-5 can be done in parallel or any order. All are backward-compatible (no data migration needed).
